    PROCEDURE MoveICJnlDimToHandled@8(TableID@1003 : Integer;NewTableID@1002 : Integer;TransactionNo@1001 : Integer;ICPartner@1000 : Code[20];LineNoFilter@1006 : Boolean;LineNo@1007 : Integer);
    VAR
      InOutboxJnlLineDim@1004 : Record 423;
      TempInOutboxJnlLineDim@1005 : TEMPORARY Record 423;
    BEGIN
      InOutboxJnlLineDim.SETRANGE("Table ID",TableID);
      InOutboxJnlLineDim.SETRANGE("Transaction No.",TransactionNo);
      InOutboxJnlLineDim.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code",ICPartner);
      IF LineNoFilter THEN
        InOutboxJnlLineDim.SETRANGE("Line No.",LineNo);
      IF InOutboxJnlLineDim.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
      REPEAT
          TempInOutboxJnlLineDim := InOutboxJnlLineDim;
          InOutboxJnlLineDim.DELETE;
          TempInOutboxJnlLineDim."Table ID" := NewTableID;
          TempInOutboxJnlLineDim.INSERT;
        UNTIL InOutboxJnlLineDim.NEXT = 0;
        IF TempInOutboxJnlLineDim.FIND('-') THEN
          REPEAT
            InOutboxJnlLineDim := TempInOutboxJnlLineDim;
            InOutboxJnlLineDim.INSERT;
          UNTIL TempInOutboxJnlLineDim.NEXT = 0;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE MoveOutboxTransToHandledOutbox@37(VAR ICOutboxTrans@1000 : Record 414);
    VAR
      HandledICOutboxTrans@1015 : Record 416;
      ICOutboxJnlLine@1001 : Record 415;
      HandledICOutboxJnlLine@1002 : Record 417;
      ICOutboxSalesHdr@1003 : Record 426;
      HandledICOutboxSalesHdr@1004 : Record 430;
      ICOutboxSalesLine@1005 : Record 427;
      HandledICOutboxSalesLine@1006 : Record 431;
      ICOutboxPurchHdr@1010 : Record 428;
      HandledICOutboxPurchHdr@1009 : Record 432;
      ICOutboxPurchLine@1008 : Record 429;
      HandledICOutboxPurchLine@1007 : Record 433;
      ICInOutJnlLineDim@1011 : Record 423;
      HandledICInOutJnlLineDim@1013 : Record 423;
      ICDocDim@1012 : Record 442;
      HandledICDocDim@1014 : Record 442;
      ICCommentLine@1016 : Record 424;
      HandledICCommentLine@1017 : Record 424;
      "<< 3LI LOCALS >>"@8053001 : Integer;
      RecOutBoxCollectReceipt_L@8053000 : Record 8053288;
      RecHandOutBoxCollectReceipt_L@8053002 : Record 8053289;
      RecOutBoxCollectRcptLine_L@8053003 : Record 8053292;
      RecHandOutBoxCollectRcptLine_L@8053004 : Record 8053293;
      RecOutBoxAnalysis_L@8053005 : Record 8053296;
      RecHandOutBoxAnalysis_L@8053006 : Record 8053297;
      RecOutboxWeighing_L@8053007 : Record 8053300;
      RecHandOutboxWeighing_L@8053008 : Record 8053301;
      RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L@8053009 : Record 8053338;
      RecICHandledOutboxPostDel_L@8053010 : Record 8053339;
      RecICOutboxPostedDelAnal_L@8053011 : Record 8053342;
      RecICHandOutPostDelAnal_L@8053012 : Record 8053343;
      RecIcOutboxItemJnlLn_L@1000000000 : Record 8053349;
      RecIcHandledOutBxItemJnlLn_L@1000000001 : Record 8053460;
      RecIcOutboxShipOrder_L@1000000002 : Record 8053469;
      RecIcHandledOutBxShipOrder_L@1000000003 : Record 8053470;
    BEGIN
      ICOutboxJnlLine.SETRANGE("Transaction No.",ICOutboxTrans."Transaction No.");
      IF ICOutboxJnlLine.FIND('-') THEN
        REPEAT
          HandledICOutboxJnlLine.TRANSFERFIELDS(ICOutboxJnlLine,TRUE);
          HandledICOutboxJnlLine.INSERT;
          ICInOutJnlLineDim.SETRANGE("Table ID",DATABASE::"IC Outbox Jnl. Line");
          ICInOutJnlLineDim.SETRANGE("Transaction No.",ICOutboxJnlLine."Transaction No.");
          ICInOutJnlLineDim.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code",ICOutboxJnlLine."IC Partner Code");
          ICInOutJnlLineDim.SETRANGE("Transaction Source",ICOutboxJnlLine."Transaction Source");
          ICInOutJnlLineDim.SETRANGE("Line No.",ICOutboxJnlLine."Line No.");
          IF ICInOutJnlLineDim.FIND('-') THEN
            REPEAT
              HandledICInOutJnlLineDim := ICInOutJnlLineDim;
              HandledICInOutJnlLineDim."Table ID" := DATABASE::"Handled IC Outbox Jnl. Line";
              HandledICInOutJnlLineDim.INSERT;
              ICInOutJnlLineDim.DELETE;
            UNTIL ICInOutJnlLineDim.NEXT = 0;
          ICOutboxJnlLine.DELETE;
        UNTIL ICOutboxJnlLine.NEXT = 0;

      ICOutboxSalesHdr.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.",ICOutboxTrans."Transaction No.");
      IF ICOutboxSalesHdr.FIND('-') THEN
        REPEAT
          HandledICOutboxSalesHdr.TRANSFERFIELDS(ICOutboxSalesHdr,TRUE);
          HandledICOutboxSalesHdr.INSERT;
          ICDocDim.SETRANGE("Table ID",DATABASE::"IC Outbox Sales Header");
          ICDocDim.SETRANGE("Transaction No.",ICOutboxSalesHdr."IC Transaction No.");
          ICDocDim.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code",ICOutboxSalesHdr."IC Partner Code");
          ICDocDim.SETRANGE("Transaction Source",ICOutboxSalesHdr."Transaction Source");
          ICDocDim.SETRANGE("Line No.",0);
          IF ICDocDim.FIND('-') THEN
            REPEAT
              HandledICDocDim.TRANSFERFIELDS(ICDocDim,TRUE);
              HandledICDocDim."Table ID" := DATABASE::"Handled IC Outbox Sales Header";
              HandledICDocDim.INSERT;
              ICDocDim.DELETE;
            UNTIL ICDocDim.NEXT = 0;
          ICOutboxSalesLine.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.",ICOutboxSalesHdr."IC Transaction No.");
          ICOutboxSalesLine.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code",ICOutboxSalesHdr."IC Partner Code");
          ICOutboxSalesLine.SETRANGE("Transaction Source",ICOutboxSalesHdr."Transaction Source");
          IF ICOutboxSalesLine.FIND('-') THEN
            REPEAT
              HandledICOutboxSalesLine.TRANSFERFIELDS(ICOutboxSalesLine,TRUE);
              HandledICOutboxSalesLine.INSERT;
              ICDocDim.SETRANGE("Table ID",DATABASE::"IC Outbox Sales Line");
              ICDocDim.SETRANGE("Transaction No.",ICOutboxSalesHdr."IC Transaction No.");
              ICDocDim.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code",ICOutboxSalesHdr."IC Partner Code");
              ICDocDim.SETRANGE("Transaction Source",ICOutboxSalesHdr."Transaction Source");
              ICDocDim.SETRANGE("Line No.",ICOutboxSalesLine."Line No.");
              IF ICDocDim.FIND('-') THEN
                REPEAT
                  HandledICDocDim.TRANSFERFIELDS(ICDocDim,TRUE);
                  HandledICDocDim."Table ID" := DATABASE::"Handled IC Outbox Sales Line";
                  HandledICDocDim.INSERT;
                  ICDocDim.DELETE;
                UNTIL ICDocDim.NEXT = 0;
              ICOutboxSalesLine.DELETE;
            UNTIL ICOutboxSalesLine.NEXT = 0;
          ICOutboxSalesHdr.DELETE;
        UNTIL ICOutboxSalesHdr.NEXT = 0;

      ICOutboxPurchHdr.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.",ICOutboxTrans."Transaction No.");
      IF ICOutboxPurchHdr.FIND('-') THEN
        REPEAT
          HandledICOutboxPurchHdr.TRANSFERFIELDS(ICOutboxPurchHdr,TRUE);
          HandledICOutboxPurchHdr.INSERT;
          ICDocDim.SETRANGE("Table ID",DATABASE::"IC Outbox Purchase Header");
          ICDocDim.SETRANGE("Transaction No.",ICOutboxPurchHdr."IC Transaction No.");
          ICDocDim.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code",ICOutboxPurchHdr."IC Partner Code");
          ICDocDim.SETRANGE("Transaction Source",ICOutboxPurchHdr."Transaction Source");
          ICDocDim.SETRANGE("Line No.",0);
          IF ICDocDim.FIND('-') THEN
            REPEAT
              HandledICDocDim.TRANSFERFIELDS(ICDocDim,TRUE);
              HandledICDocDim."Table ID" := DATABASE::"Handled IC Outbox Purch. Hdr";
              HandledICDocDim.INSERT;
              ICDocDim.DELETE;
            UNTIL ICDocDim.NEXT = 0;
          ICOutboxPurchLine.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.",ICOutboxPurchHdr."IC Transaction No.");
          ICOutboxPurchLine.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code",ICOutboxPurchHdr."IC Partner Code");
          ICOutboxPurchLine.SETRANGE("Transaction Source",ICOutboxPurchHdr."Transaction Source");
          IF ICOutboxPurchLine.FIND('-') THEN
            REPEAT
              HandledICOutboxPurchLine.TRANSFERFIELDS(ICOutboxPurchLine,TRUE);
              HandledICOutboxPurchLine.INSERT;
              ICDocDim.SETRANGE("Table ID",DATABASE::"IC Outbox Purchase Line");
              ICDocDim.SETRANGE("Transaction No.",ICOutboxPurchHdr."IC Transaction No.");
              ICDocDim.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code",ICOutboxPurchHdr."IC Partner Code");
              ICDocDim.SETRANGE("Transaction Source",ICOutboxPurchHdr."Transaction Source");
              ICDocDim.SETRANGE("Line No.",ICOutboxPurchLine."Line No.");
              IF ICDocDim.FIND('-') THEN
                REPEAT
                  HandledICDocDim.TRANSFERFIELDS(ICDocDim,TRUE);
                  HandledICDocDim."Table ID" := DATABASE::"Handled IC Outbox Purch. Line";
                  HandledICDocDim.INSERT;
                  ICDocDim.DELETE;
                UNTIL ICDocDim.NEXT = 0;
              ICOutboxPurchLine.DELETE;
            UNTIL ICOutboxPurchLine.NEXT = 0;
          ICOutboxPurchHdr.DELETE;
        UNTIL ICOutboxPurchHdr.NEXT = 0;

      HandledICOutboxTrans.TRANSFERFIELDS(ICOutboxTrans,TRUE);
      CASE ICOutboxTrans."Line Action" OF
        ICOutboxTrans."Line Action"::"Send to IC Partner":
          IF ICOutboxTrans."Transaction Source" = ICOutboxTrans."Transaction Source"::"Created by Current Company" THEN
            HandledICOutboxTrans.Status := HandledICOutboxTrans.Status::"Sent to IC Partner"
          ELSE
            HandledICOutboxTrans.Status := HandledICOutboxTrans.Status::"Rejection Sent to IC Partner";
        ICOutboxTrans."Line Action"::Cancel:
          HandledICOutboxTrans.Status := HandledICOutboxTrans.Status::Cancelled;
      END;
      HandledICOutboxTrans.INSERT;
      ICOutboxTrans.DELETE;

      ICCommentLine.SETRANGE("Table Name",ICCommentLine."Table Name"::"IC Outbox Transaction");
      ICCommentLine.SETRANGE("Transaction No.",ICOutboxTrans."Transaction No.");
      ICCommentLine.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code",ICOutboxTrans."IC Partner Code");
      ICCommentLine.SETRANGE("Transaction Source",ICOutboxTrans."Transaction Source");
      IF ICCommentLine.FIND('-') THEN
        REPEAT
          HandledICCommentLine := ICCommentLine;
          HandledICCommentLine."Table Name" := HandledICCommentLine."Table Name"::"Handled IC Outbox Transaction";
          HandledICCommentLine.INSERT;
          ICCommentLine.DELETE;
        UNTIL ICCommentLine.NEXT = 0;

      // --> 20131115-Gen15/001-CMC02.02.00-FBE01
      // Transfere l'ent^te et lignes de r'ception c'r'ales, analyses et pes'es de la boite d'envoi vers
      // l'ent^te et lignes de r'ception c'r'ales, analyses et pes'es  boOte d'envoi g'r'e
      RecOutBoxCollectReceipt_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.",ICOutboxTrans."Transaction No.");
      IF RecOutBoxCollectReceipt_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          RecHandOutBoxCollectReceipt_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecOutBoxCollectReceipt_L);
          RecHandOutBoxCollectReceipt_L.INSERT;
          RecOutBoxCollectReceipt_L.DELETE;
          // Lignes
          RecOutBoxCollectRcptLine_L.SETCURRENTKEY("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source");
          RecOutBoxCollectRcptLine_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.",RecOutBoxCollectReceipt_L."IC Transaction No.");
          RecOutBoxCollectRcptLine_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code",RecOutBoxCollectReceipt_L."IC Partner Code");
          RecOutBoxCollectRcptLine_L.SETRANGE("Transaction Source",RecOutBoxCollectReceipt_L."Transaction Source");
          IF RecOutBoxCollectRcptLine_L.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            RecHandOutBoxCollectRcptLine_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecOutBoxCollectRcptLine_L);
            RecHandOutBoxCollectRcptLine_L.INSERT;
            RecOutBoxCollectRcptLine_L.DELETE;
          UNTIL RecOutBoxCollectRcptLine_L.NEXT = 0;
          //Analyses
          RecOutBoxAnalysis_L.SETCURRENTKEY("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source","Analysis Code");
          RecOutBoxAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.",RecOutBoxCollectReceipt_L."IC Transaction No.");
          RecOutBoxAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code",RecOutBoxCollectReceipt_L."IC Partner Code");
          RecOutBoxAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Transaction Source",RecOutBoxCollectReceipt_L."Transaction Source");
            IF RecOutBoxAnalysis_L.FINDSET THEN
            REPEAT
              RecHandOutBoxAnalysis_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecOutBoxAnalysis_L);
              RecHandOutBoxAnalysis_L.INSERT;
              RecOutBoxAnalysis_L.DELETE;
            UNTIL RecOutBoxAnalysis_L.NEXT =0;
          // Pes'es
          RecOutboxWeighing_L.SETCURRENTKEY("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source");
          RecOutboxWeighing_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.",RecOutBoxCollectReceipt_L."IC Transaction No.");
          RecOutboxWeighing_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code",RecOutBoxCollectReceipt_L."IC Partner Code");
          RecOutboxWeighing_L.SETRANGE("Transaction Source",RecOutBoxCollectReceipt_L."Transaction Source");
            IF RecOutboxWeighing_L.FINDSET THEN
            REPEAT
              RecHandOutboxWeighing_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecOutboxWeighing_L);
              RecHandOutboxWeighing_L.INSERT;
              RecOutboxWeighing_L.DELETE;
            UNTIL RecOutboxWeighing_L.NEXT =0;

        UNTIL RecOutBoxCollectReceipt_L.NEXT = 0;
      // <-- 20131115-Gen15/001-CMC02.02.00-FBE01

      // --> 20140114-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-DRN01
      // Transfert de la livraison collecte de la boite d'envoi IC vers la boOte d'envoi IC g'r'e
      RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.",ICOutboxTrans."Transaction No.");
      IF RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          RecICHandledOutboxPostDel_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L);
          RecICHandledOutboxPostDel_L.INSERT;
          // Analyses
          RecICOutboxPostedDelAnal_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.",RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."IC Transaction No.");
          RecICOutboxPostedDelAnal_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code",RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."IC Partner Code");
          RecICOutboxPostedDelAnal_L.SETRANGE("Transaction Source",RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."Transaction Source");
            IF RecICOutboxPostedDelAnal_L.FINDSET THEN
            REPEAT
              RecICHandOutPostDelAnal_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecICOutboxPostedDelAnal_L);
              RecICHandOutPostDelAnal_L.INSERT;
            UNTIL RecICOutboxPostedDelAnal_L.NEXT =0;
          // Pes'e
          RecOutboxWeighing_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.",RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."IC Transaction No.");
          RecOutboxWeighing_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code",RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."IC Partner Code");
          RecOutboxWeighing_L.SETRANGE("Transaction Source",RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."Transaction Source");
          IF RecOutboxWeighing_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            RecHandOutboxWeighing_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecOutboxWeighing_L);
            RecHandOutboxWeighing_L.INSERT;
          END;
          RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L.DELETE(TRUE);
        UNTIL RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L.NEXT = 0;
      // <-- 20140114-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-DRN01

      // --> 20140129-Gen15/001-CMC02.02.00-FBE01
      // Transfere les lignes feuille article de la boite d'envoi vers les lignes feuille article boOte d'envoi g'r'e
      RecIcOutboxItemJnlLn_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.",ICOutboxTrans."Transaction No.");
      IF RecIcOutboxItemJnlLn_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          RecIcHandledOutBxItemJnlLn_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecIcOutboxItemJnlLn_L);
          RecIcHandledOutBxItemJnlLn_L.INSERT;
          RecIcOutboxItemJnlLn_L.DELETE(TRUE);
        UNTIL RecIcOutboxItemJnlLn_L.NEXT = 0;
      // <-- 20140129-Gen15/001-CMC02.02.00-FBE01

      // --> 20140304-Gen15/001-CMC02.02.00-FBE01
      // Transfere les ordres de chargement de la boite d'envoi vers les ordres de chargement boOte d'envoi g'r'e
      RecIcOutboxShipOrder_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.",ICOutboxTrans."Transaction No.");
      IF RecIcOutboxShipOrder_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          RecIcHandledOutBxShipOrder_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecIcOutboxShipOrder_L);
          RecIcHandledOutBxShipOrder_L.INSERT;
          RecIcOutboxShipOrder_L.DELETE(TRUE);
        UNTIL RecIcOutboxShipOrder_L.NEXT = 0;
      // <-- 20140304-Gen15/001-CMC02.02.00-FBE01
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateLineDim@34(TableNo@1000 : Integer;DocNo@1001 : Code[20];DocType@1002 : 'Quote,Order,Invoice,Credit Memo,Blanket Order,Return Order';LineNo@1009 : Integer);
    VAR
      DocDimHeader@1004 : Record 357;
      DocDimLine@1005 : Record 357;
      DocDim@1006 : Record 357;
      LineTableNo@1003 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      DocDimHeader.RESET;
      DocDimHeader.SETRANGE("Table ID",TableNo);
      DocDimHeader.SETRANGE("Document No.", DocNo);
      DocDimHeader.SETRANGE("Document Type", DocType);
      DocDimHeader.SETRANGE("Line No.",0);
      IF NOT DocDimHeader.FIND('-') THEN
        EXIT;

      CASE TableNo OF
        DATABASE::"Sales Header":
          LineTableNo := DATABASE::"Sales Line";
        DATABASE::"Purchase Header":
          LineTableNo := DATABASE::"Purchase Line";
      END;
      DocDimLine.RESET;
      DocDimLine.SETRANGE("Table ID",LineTableNo);
      DocDimLine.SETRANGE("Document No.", DocDimHeader."Document No.");
      DocDimLine.SETRANGE("Document Type", DocDimHeader."Document Type");
      DocDimLine.SETRANGE("Line No.",LineNo);

      REPEAT
        DocDimLine.SETRANGE("Dimension Code", DocDimHeader."Dimension Code");
        IF DocDimLine.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
          IF DocDimHeader."Dimension Value Code" <> DocDimLine."Dimension Value Code"
          THEN BEGIN
            DocDimLine."Dimension Value Code" := DocDimHeader."Dimension Value Code";
            DocDimLine.MODIFY(TRUE);
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          DocDim.INIT;
          DocDim."Table ID" := LineTableNo;
          DocDim."Document Type" := DocDimHeader."Document Type";
          DocDim."Document No." := DocDimHeader."Document No.";
          DocDim."Line No." := LineNo;
          DocDim."Dimension Code" := DocDimHeader."Dimension Code";
          DocDim."Dimension Value Code" := DocDimHeader."Dimension Value Code";
          DocDim.INSERT(TRUE);
        END;
      UNTIL DocDimHeader.NEXT = 0;
    END;

    PROCEDURE "<<3LI FUNCTIONS>>"@1180250002();
    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateOutboxCollectDocAction@8053000(VAR RecCMCPostedReceipt_P@8053000 : Record 8052995;DocActionType_P@8053014 : 'Create,Update,Delete,Cancel,Reconcile';ValidAnalysis_P@1000000001 : Boolean);
    VAR
      RecICOutboxTransaction_L@8053009 : Record 414;
      RecICPartner_L@8053010 : Record 413;
      TransactionNo_L@8053007 : Integer;
      RecICOutboxPostReceipt_L@8053003 : Record 8053288;
      RecICOutboxPostRcptLine_L@8053004 : Record 8053292;
      RecPostedRcptLine_L@8053005 : Record 8053104;
      RecOutBoxAnalysis_L@8053013 : Record 8053296;
      RecPostedAnalysis_L@8053008 : Record 8052996;
      RecOutboxWeighing_L@8053011 : Record 8053300;
      RecPostedWeighing_L@8053012 : Record 8053095;
      cduICOutboxExport_L@8053006 : Codeunit 431;
      TempDocDim_L@8053002 : TEMPORARY Record 357;
      LinesCreated_L@8053001 : Boolean;
      AllMandatoryValueOk_L@1000000000 : Boolean;
      RecArchivedWeighing_L@1000000002 : Record 60135;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cr'e le message d'action du document Reception collecte valid'e IC dans la boOte d'envoi
      // Cr'e les enregistrements Reception collecte, lignes reception collecte, analyses et pes'es de la boite d'envoi IC
      // envoie automatiquement le message au partenaire
      // Appel dans le cdu 8052983
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecCMCPostedReceipt_P : En-t^te reception
      //   - DocActionType_P : Option = Create,Update,Delete,Cancel,Reconcile  //TODO
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // cr'e la transaction dans la boite d'envoi IC
      WITH RecCMCPostedReceipt_P DO BEGIN
        TESTFIELD( "IC Partner Code" );
        RecICPartner_L.GET( "IC Partner Code");
        IF RecICPartner_L."Cereal Flow" = RecICPartner_L."Cereal Flow"::"No IC Transfer" THEN
          EXIT;
        IF (RecICPartner_L."Cereal Flow" IN [RecICPartner_L."Cereal Flow"::"File Location",RecICPartner_L."Cereal Flow"::"E-Mail"]) THEN
          IF ValidAnalysis_P THEN
            DocActionType_P := DocActionType_P::Create
          ELSE
            EXIT;

        RecICPartner_L.TESTFIELD(Blocked,FALSE);
        GLSetup.LOCKTABLE;
        GetGLSetup;
        TransactionNo_L := GLSetup."Last IC Transaction No." + 1;
        GLSetup."Last IC Transaction No." := TransactionNo_L;
        GLSetup.MODIFY;

        RecICOutboxTransaction_L.INIT;
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Transaction No."    := TransactionNo_L;
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."IC Partner Code"    := "IC Partner Code";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Transaction Source" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Transaction Source":: "Created by Current Company";
        CASE RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."Receipt Type" OF
          RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."Receipt Type"::Receipt :
            BEGIN
              RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Source Type" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Source Type"::"Purchase Collect Document";
              RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Type" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Type"::"Collect Receipt";
            END;
          RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."Receipt Type"::Resumption :
            BEGIN
              RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Source Type" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Source Type"::"Purchase Collect Document";
              RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Type" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Type"::"Collect Resumption Receipt";
            END;
          RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."Receipt Type"::Transfert:
            BEGIN
              RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Source Type" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Source Type"::"Transfer Collect Document";
              RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Type" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Type"::"Collect Transfer Receipt";
              IF DocActionType_P IN [DocActionType_P::Cancel,DocActionType_P::Reconcile] THEN
                TestPendingICInboxTransfer(RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Source Type", RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Type","No.");
            END;
        END;
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document No."       := "No.";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Posting Date"       := "Posting Date";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Date"      := "Posting Date";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Line Action"        := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Line Action"::"Send to IC Partner" ;
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Action Type" := DocActionType_P;
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L.INSERT;
      END;

      // Cr'e l'entete de r'ception c'r'ales dans la boite de d'envoi IC,
      RecICOutboxPostReceipt_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecCMCPostedReceipt_P);
      RecICOutboxPostReceipt_L."IC Transaction No."  := TransactionNo_L;
      RecICOutboxPostReceipt_L."Transaction Source"  := RecICOutboxPostReceipt_L."Transaction Source"::"Created by Current Company";
      RecICOutboxPostReceipt_L."IC Partner Code"     := RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."IC Partner Code";
      RecICOutboxPostReceipt_L."Valid Receipt"       := ValidAnalysis_P;
      // --> 20150703-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
      RecICOutboxPostReceipt_L."Weighing 1 No." := '';
      RecICOutboxPostReceipt_L."Weighing 2 No." := '';
      // <-- 20150703-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
      RecICOutboxPostReceipt_L.INSERT;

      // cr'e les lignes de r'ception c'r'ales dans la boite d'envoi IC
      WITH RecICOutboxPostRcptLine_L DO BEGIN
        RecPostedRcptLine_L.SETRANGE("Receipt Type",RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."Receipt Type");
        RecPostedRcptLine_L.SETRANGE("No.",RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."No.");
        IF RecPostedRcptLine_L.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            INIT;
            TRANSFERFIELDS(RecPostedRcptLine_L);
            "IC Transaction No." := TransactionNo_L;
            "Transaction Source" := RecICOutboxPostRcptLine_L."Transaction Source"::"Created by Current Company";
            "IC Partner Code"    := RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."IC Partner Code";
            INSERT;
          UNTIL RecPostedRcptLine_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;

      //Analyses
      WITH RecOutBoxAnalysis_L DO BEGIN
        RecPostedAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Receipt Type",RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."Receipt Type");
        RecPostedAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Receipt No.",RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."No.");
        IF RecPostedAnalysis_L.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            INIT;
            TRANSFERFIELDS(RecPostedAnalysis_L);
            "IC Transaction No." := TransactionNo_L;
            "Transaction Source" :=  "Transaction Source"::"Created by Current Company";
            "IC Partner Code"    := RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."IC Partner Code";
            INSERT;
          UNTIL RecPostedAnalysis_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;

      //Pes'es
      WITH RecOutboxWeighing_L DO BEGIN
        // --> 20210521-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3555-CJY01
        {BEFORE
        RecPostedWeighing_L.GET(RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."Weighing No.");
        AFTER}
        IF RecPostedWeighing_L.GET(RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."Weighing No.") THEN BEGIN
        // <-- 20210521-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3555-CJY01
        // enregistrment du n› de pes'e ici car non pr'sent dans CMC Post receipt
        //Todo -> Pas besoin car le N› ticket bascule est stock' dans l'enregistrement de pes'e
        //RecICOutboxPostReceipt_L."Manual Weighing No." := RecPostedWeighing_L."Manual Weighing No.";
        //RecICOutboxPostReceipt_L.MODIFY;
        INIT;
        TRANSFERFIELDS(RecPostedWeighing_L);
        "IC Transaction No." := TransactionNo_L;
        "Transaction Source" :=  "Transaction Source"::"Created by Current Company";
        "IC Partner Code" := RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."IC Partner Code";
        // --> 20150703-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
        "Weighing 1 No." := '';
        "Weighing 2 No." := '';
        // <-- 20150703-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
        INSERT;
        // --> 20210521-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3555-CJY01
        END
        ELSE BEGIN
          IF RecArchivedWeighing_L.GET(RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."Weighing No.") THEN BEGIN
            INIT;
            TRANSFERFIELDS(RecArchivedWeighing_L);
            "IC Transaction No." := TransactionNo_L;
            "Transaction Source" :=  "Transaction Source"::"Created by Current Company";
            "IC Partner Code" := RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."IC Partner Code";
            "Weighing 1 No." := '';
            "Weighing 2 No." := '';
            INSERT;
          END;
        END;
        // <-- 20210521-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3555-CJY01

      END;

      // Mise . jour du statut de la r'ception
      IF RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."IC Status" = RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."IC Status"::New THEN BEGIN
        RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."IC Status" := RecCMCPostedReceipt_P."IC Status"::Sent;
      END;

      // Envoi automatique vers la boite de reception IC partenaire
      cduICOutboxExport_L.AutoSendToInternalPartner(RecICOutboxTransaction_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateOutboxDeliveryDocAction@8053007(VAR RecPostedDelivery_P@8053000 : Record 8053014;DocActionType_P@8053001 : 'Create,Update,Delete,Cancel,Reconcile');
    VAR
      RecICOutboxTransaction_L@8053009 : Record 414;
      RecICPartner_L@8053010 : Record 413;
      TransactionNo_L@8053007 : Integer;
      RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L@8053003 : Record 8053338;
      RecICOutBoxPostedDelAnal_L@8053013 : Record 8053342;
      RecPostedDeliveryAnalysis_L@8053008 : Record 8053015;
      RecICOutboxPostedWeighing_L@8053011 : Record 8053300;
      RecPostedWeighing_L@8053012 : Record 8053095;
      cduICOutboxExport_L@8053006 : Codeunit 431;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cr'e le message d'action de la livraison collecte dans la boOte d'envoi IC
      // et l'envoie vers la boOte de r'ception IC du partenaire
      // Appel dans le cdu 8052988 et pge 8053625 et Cdu 8052987
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecPostedDelivery_P : Livraison ou Livraison transfert ind'pendant
      //   - DocActionType_P : Option = Create,Update,Delete,Cancel,Reconcile
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      WITH RecPostedDelivery_P DO BEGIN
        TESTFIELD("IC Partner Code");
        RecICPartner_L.GET("IC Partner Code");
        IF RecICPartner_L."Cereal Flow" = RecICPartner_L."Cereal Flow"::"No IC Transfer" THEN
          EXIT;
        RecICPartner_L.TESTFIELD(Blocked,FALSE);
        GLSetup.LOCKTABLE;
        GetGLSetup;
        TransactionNo_L := GLSetup."Last IC Transaction No." + 1;
        GLSetup."Last IC Transaction No." := TransactionNo_L;
        GLSetup.MODIFY;

        // Cr'ation de la transaction dans la boite d'envoi IC
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L.INIT;
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Transaction No." := TransactionNo_L;
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."IC Partner Code" := "IC Partner Code";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Transaction Source" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Transaction Source":: "Created by Current Company";
        CASE "Delivery Type" OF
          // Cas des livraisons collecte
          "Delivery Type"::Delivery :
            BEGIN
              RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Source Type" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Source Type"::"Sales Collect Document";
              RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Type" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Type"::"Collect Delivery";
            END;
          // Cas des livraisons transfert collecte
          "Delivery Type"::Transfert :
            BEGIN
              RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Source Type" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Source Type"::"Transfer Collect Document";
              RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Type" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Type"::"Collect Transfer Delivery";
              IF DocActionType_P IN [DocActionType_P::Cancel,DocActionType_P::Reconcile] THEN
                TestPendingICInboxTransfer(RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Source Type",RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Type","No.");
            END;
        END;
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document No." := "No.";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Posting Date" := "Posting Date";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Date" := "Posting Date";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Line Action" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Line Action"::"Send to IC Partner" ;
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Action Type" := DocActionType_P;
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."On Behalf Of Adjustment" := "On Behalf Of Adjustment";
        // Cas de la r'gule pour le compte de silo partag' soci't' de gestion et 2 soci't's utilisatrices
        // Cas du transfert de propri't' o- le code partenaire IC et le code partenaire IC origine sont identiques
        // -> Soci't' utilisatrice qui doit . la soci't' de gestion du silo partag'
        IF "IC Partner Code" <> "IC Partner-From Code" THEN
          RecICOutboxTransaction_L."On Behalf Of Partner-From Code" := "IC Partner-From Code";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Ownership Transfer" := "Ownership Transfer";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L.INSERT;

        // Cr'ation de la livraison collecte de la boite d'envoi IC
        RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecPostedDelivery_P);
        RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."IC Transaction No." := TransactionNo_L;
        RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."Transaction Source" :=
          RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."Transaction Source"::"Created by Current Company";
        RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."IC Partner Code" := RecPostedDelivery_P."IC Partner Code";
        // --> 20150703-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
        RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."Weighing 1 No." := '';
        RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."Weighing 2 No." := '';
        // <-- 20150703-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
        // --> 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
        RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."Actual Location Code" := "Location Code";
        // <-- 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
        RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L.INSERT;

        // Cr'ation des analyses de la livraison collecte de la boite d'envoi IC
        // --> 20150430-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-2944-FBE01
        IF DocActionType_P <> DocActionType_P::Update THEN
        // <-- 20150430-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-2944-FBE01
          WITH RecICOutBoxPostedDelAnal_L DO BEGIN
            RecPostedDeliveryAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Delivery Type",RecPostedDelivery_P."Delivery Type");
            RecPostedDeliveryAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Delivery No.",RecPostedDelivery_P."No.");
            IF RecPostedDeliveryAnalysis_L.FINDSET THEN
              REPEAT
                INIT;
                TRANSFERFIELDS(RecPostedDeliveryAnalysis_L);
                "IC Transaction No." := TransactionNo_L;
                "Transaction Source" := "Transaction Source"::"Created by Current Company";
                "IC Partner Code" := RecPostedDelivery_P."IC Partner Code";
                INSERT;
              UNTIL RecPostedDeliveryAnalysis_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;

        // Cr'ation de la pes'e de la livraison collecte de la boite d'envoi IC
        // --> 20150430-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-2944-FBE01
        IF DocActionType_P <> DocActionType_P::Update THEN
        // <-- 20150430-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-2944-FBE01
          WITH RecICOutboxPostedWeighing_L DO BEGIN
            // Cas du transfert de propri't' -> pas d'enregistrement de pes'e
            IF RecPostedWeighing_L.GET(RecPostedDelivery_P."Weighing No.") THEN BEGIN
              INIT;
              TRANSFERFIELDS(RecPostedWeighing_L);
              "IC Transaction No." := TransactionNo_L;
              "Transaction Source" :=  "Transaction Source"::"Created by Current Company";
              "IC Partner Code" := RecPostedDelivery_P."IC Partner Code";
              // --> 20150703-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
              "Weighing 1 No." := '';
              "Weighing 2 No." := '';
              // <-- 20150703-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
              INSERT;
            END;
          END;
      END;

      // Envoi automatique vers la boite de r'ception IC du partenaire
      cduICOutboxExport_L.AutoSendToInternalPartner(RecICOutboxTransaction_L);

      // Cas de la r'gule pour le compte de silo partag' soci't' de gestion et 2 soci't's utilisatrices
      WITH RecPostedDelivery_P DO BEGIN
        // Cas du transfert de propri't' o- le code partenaire IC et le code partenaire IC origine sont identiques
        // -> Soci't' utilisatrice qui doit . la soci't' de gestion du silo partag'
        IF ("IC Partner-From Code" <> '') AND ("IC Partner Code" <> "IC Partner-From Code")
        THEN BEGIN
          RecICPartner_L.GET("IC Partner-From Code");
          IF RecICPartner_L."Cereal Flow" = RecICPartner_L."Cereal Flow"::"E-Mail" THEN
            EXIT;
          RecICPartner_L.TESTFIELD(Blocked,FALSE);
          TransactionNo_L := GLSetup."Last IC Transaction No." + 1;
          GLSetup."Last IC Transaction No." := TransactionNo_L;
          GLSetup.MODIFY;

          // Cr'ation de la transaction dans la boite d'envoi IC
          RecICOutboxTransaction_L.INIT;
          RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Transaction No." := TransactionNo_L;
          RecICOutboxTransaction_L."IC Partner Code" := "IC Partner-From Code";
          RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Transaction Source" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Transaction Source":: "Created by Current Company";
          RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Source Type" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Source Type"::"Transfer Collect Document";
          RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Type" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Type"::"Collect Transfer Delivery";
          IF DocActionType_P IN [DocActionType_P::Cancel,DocActionType_P::Reconcile] THEN
            TestPendingICInboxTransfer(RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Source Type",RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Type","No.");
          RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document No." := "No.";
          RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Posting Date" := "Posting Date";
          RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Date" := "Posting Date";
          RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Line Action" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Line Action"::"Send to IC Partner" ;
          RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Action Type" := DocActionType_P;
          RecICOutboxTransaction_L."On Behalf Of Adjustment" := TRUE;
          IF DocActionType_P IN [DocActionType_P::Create,DocActionType_P::Cancel] THEN
            RecICOutboxTransaction_L."On Behalf Of Partner-To Code" := RecPostedDelivery_P."IC Partner Code";
          RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Ownership Transfer" := "Ownership Transfer";
          RecICOutboxTransaction_L.INSERT;

          // Cr'ation de la livraison collecte de la boite d'envoi IC
          RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecPostedDelivery_P);
          RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."IC Transaction No." := TransactionNo_L;
          RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."Transaction Source" :=
            RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."Transaction Source"::"Created by Current Company";
          RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."IC Partner Code" := "IC Partner-From Code";
          // --> 20150703-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
          RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."Weighing 1 No." := '';
          RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."Weighing 2 No." := '';
          // <-- 20150703-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
          // --> 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
          RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L."Actual Location Code" := "Location Code";
          // <-- 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
          RecICOutboxPostedDelivery_L.INSERT;

          // Cr'ation des analyses de la livraison collecte de la boite d'envoi IC
          WITH RecICOutBoxPostedDelAnal_L DO BEGIN
            RecPostedDeliveryAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Delivery Type",RecPostedDelivery_P."Delivery Type");
            RecPostedDeliveryAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Delivery No.",RecPostedDelivery_P."No.");
            IF RecPostedDeliveryAnalysis_L.FINDSET THEN
              REPEAT
                INIT;
                TRANSFERFIELDS(RecPostedDeliveryAnalysis_L);
                "IC Transaction No." := TransactionNo_L;
                "Transaction Source" := "Transaction Source"::"Created by Current Company";
                "IC Partner Code" := RecPostedDelivery_P."IC Partner-From Code";
                INSERT;
              UNTIL RecPostedDeliveryAnalysis_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;

          // Cr'ation de la pes'e de la livraison collecte de la boite d'envoi IC
          WITH RecICOutboxPostedWeighing_L DO BEGIN
            // Cas du transfert de propri't' -> pas d'enregistrement de pes'e
            IF RecPostedWeighing_L.GET(RecPostedDelivery_P."Weighing No.") THEN BEGIN
              INIT;
              TRANSFERFIELDS(RecPostedWeighing_L);
              "IC Transaction No." := TransactionNo_L;
              "Transaction Source" :=  "Transaction Source"::"Created by Current Company";
              "IC Partner Code" := RecPostedDelivery_P."IC Partner-From Code";
              // --> 20150703-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
              "Weighing 1 No." := '';
              "Weighing 2 No." := '';
              // <-- 20150703-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
              INSERT;
            END;
          END;

          // Envoi automatique vers la boite de r'ception IC du partenaire origine
          cduICOutboxExport_L.AutoSendToInternalPartner(RecICOutboxTransaction_L);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateOutboxItemJnlAction@1000000001(VAR RecItemJnlLine_P@1000000001 : Record 83;DocActionType_P@1000000000 : 'Create,Update,Delete');
    VAR
      RecICPartner_L@1000000004 : Record 413;
      RecItemJnlLine_L@1000000007 : Record 83;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Appelle la cr'ation des lignes journal article par partenaire IC
      // Appel' par le cdu CMC Cereal Inventory Mgt.
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecItemJnlLine_P : Item Journal Line
      //   - DocActionType_P : Option = Create,Update,Delete,Cancel,Reconcile
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      WITH RecItemJnlLine_L DO BEGIN
        COPYFILTERS(RecItemJnlLine_P);
        SETCURRENTKEY("IC Partner Code");
        SETFILTER("IC Partner Code",'<>%1','');
        IF ISEMPTY THEN
          EXIT;
        IF FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            SETRANGE("IC Partner Code","IC Partner Code");
            RecICPartner_L.GET("IC Partner Code");
            IF RecICPartner_L."Cereal Flow" = RecICPartner_L."Cereal Flow"::Database THEN BEGIN
              RecICPartner_L.TESTFIELD(Blocked,FALSE);
              TESTFIELD("Location Code");
              CreateOutboxItemJnlLine(RecItemJnlLine_L,DocActionType_P);
            END;
            IF FINDLAST THEN;
            SETRANGE("IC Partner Code");
          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateOutboxItemJnlLine@1000000007(VAR RecItemJnlLine_P@1000000001 : Record 83;DocActionType_P@1000000000 : 'Create,Update,Delete');
    VAR
      RecICOutboxTransaction_L@1000000005 : Record 414;
      RecICPartner_L@1000000004 : Record 413;
      TransactionNo_L@1000000003 : Integer;
      RecIcOutboxItemJnlLine_L@1000000006 : Record 8053349;
      cduICOutboxExport_L@1000000002 : Codeunit 431;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cr'e le message d'action de la ligne journal article IC dans la boOte d'envoi
      // Cr'e les enregistrements Item journal line de la boite d'envoi
      // envoie automatiquement le message au partenaire
      // Appel dans le cdu CMC Cereal Inventory Mgt.
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecItemJnlLine_P : Item Journal Line
      //   - DocActionType_P : Option = Create,Update,Delete,Cancel,Reconcile
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // cr'e la transaction dans la boite d'envoi IC

      WITH RecItemJnlLine_P DO BEGIN
        GLSetup.LOCKTABLE;
        GetGLSetup;

        TransactionNo_L := GLSetup."Last IC Transaction No." + 1;
        GLSetup."Last IC Transaction No." := TransactionNo_L;
        GLSetup.MODIFY;

        RecICOutboxTransaction_L.INIT;
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Transaction No."    := TransactionNo_L;
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."IC Partner Code"    := "IC Partner Code";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Transaction Source" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Transaction Source":: "Created by Current Company";

        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Source Type" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Source Type"::"Item Journal Line";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Type" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Type"::"Inventory line";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document No." := RecItemJnlLine_P."Document No.";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Posting Date" := "Posting Date";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Date" := "Posting Date";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Line Action" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Line Action"::"Send to IC Partner" ;
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Action Type" := DocActionType_P;
        IF NOT RecICOutboxTransaction_L.INSERT THEN ;

        FINDSET;
        REPEAT
        // cr'e la ligne journal article de la boite d'envoi
          RecIcOutboxItemJnlLine_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecItemJnlLine_P);
          RecIcOutboxItemJnlLine_L."IC Transaction No."  := TransactionNo_L;
          RecIcOutboxItemJnlLine_L."Transaction Source"  := RecIcOutboxItemJnlLine_L."Transaction Source"::"Created by Current Company";
          RecIcOutboxItemJnlLine_L."IC Partner Code"     := RecItemJnlLine_P."IC Partner Code";
          RecIcOutboxItemJnlLine_L.INSERT;
        UNTIL NEXT = 0;

        // Envoi automatique vers la boite de r'ception IC du partenaire
        cduICOutboxExport_L.AutoSendToInternalPartner(RecICOutboxTransaction_L);

      END;  // With
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateOutboxShippingOrder@1000000005(VAR RecShippingOrder_P@1000000001 : Record 8053265;DocActionType_P@1000000000 : 'Create,Update,Delete');
    VAR
      RecICOutboxTransaction_L@1000000004 : Record 414;
      RecIcOutboxShipOrder_L@1000000005 : Record 8053469;
      RecICPartner_L@1000000003 : Record 413;
      TransactionNo_L@1000000002 : Integer;
      cduICOutboxExport_L@1000000006 : Codeunit 431;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cr'e le message d'action Ordre de chargement dans la Boite d'envoi IC
      // Cr'e l'ordre de chargement IC outbox
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecShippingOrder_P : L'ordre de chargement
      //   - DocActionType_P : Option = Create,Update,Delete,Cancel,Reconcile
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // cr'e la transaction dans la boite d'envoi IC
      WITH RecShippingOrder_P DO BEGIN
        TESTFIELD(Status,Status::Released);
        TESTFIELD("Send IC Document",TRUE);
        TESTFIELD("IC Partner Code");
        // --> 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        IF DocActionType_P = DocActionType_P :: Create THEN
        // <-- 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        TESTFIELD("IC Status","IC Status"::New);
        TESTFIELD("IC Direction" ,"IC Direction"::Outgoing);
        // --> 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
        TESTFIELD(RecShippingOrder_P."On Behalf Of",TRUE);
        IF ("From Type" = "From Type"::Location) AND ("Destination Type" = "Destination Type"::Location) THEN
          TESTFIELD("Shared Silo",TRUE);
        // <-- 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
        RecICPartner_L.GET("IC Partner Code");
        IF RecICPartner_L."Cereal Flow" = RecICPartner_L."Cereal Flow"::"E-Mail" THEN
          EXIT;
        RecICPartner_L.TESTFIELD(Blocked,FALSE);
        GLSetup.LOCKTABLE;
        GetGLSetup;
        TransactionNo_L := GLSetup."Last IC Transaction No." + 1;
        GLSetup."Last IC Transaction No." := TransactionNo_L;
        GLSetup.MODIFY;

        // Cr'ation de la transaction dans la boite d'envoi IC
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L.INIT;
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Transaction No." := TransactionNo_L;
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."IC Partner Code" := "IC Partner Code";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Transaction Source" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Transaction Source":: "Created by Current Company";

        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Source Type" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Source Type"::"Shipping Document";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Type" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Type"::"Shipping Order";

        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document No." := "No.";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Posting Date" := "Creation Date";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Date" := "Creation Date";
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Line Action" := RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Line Action"::"Send to IC Partner" ;
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L."Document Action Type" := DocActionType_P;
        RecICOutboxTransaction_L.INSERT;

        RecIcOutboxShipOrder_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecShippingOrder_P);
        RecIcOutboxShipOrder_L."IC Transaction No." := TransactionNo_L;
        RecIcOutboxShipOrder_L."IC Partner Code"    := "IC Partner Code";
        RecIcOutboxShipOrder_L."Transaction Source" :=  RecIcOutboxShipOrder_L."Transaction Source"::"Created by Current Company";
        RecIcOutboxShipOrder_L.INSERT;
        // Mise . jour du statut de l'ordre de transfert
        IF RecShippingOrder_P."IC Status" = RecShippingOrder_P."IC Status"::New THEN BEGIN
          RecShippingOrder_P."IC Status" := RecShippingOrder_P."IC Status"::Sent;
        END;
        // Envoi automatique vers la boite de r'ception IC du partenaire
        cduICOutboxExport_L.AutoSendToInternalPartner(RecICOutboxTransaction_L);
        // --> 20150619-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
        MESSAGE(Text8053012);
        // <-- 20150619-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01

      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE OutBoxCollectHeaderToInBox@8053002(VAR RecICInboxTrans_P@8053002 : Record 418;VAR RecOutBoxPostReceipt_P@8053000 : Record 8053288;VAR RecInBoxPostReceipt_P@8053001 : Record 8053290);
    VAR
      RecICPartner_L@8053003 : Record 413;
      RecVendor_L@8053004 : Record 23;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cr'e l'ent^te Reception collecte IC dans la boOte de reception du partenaire
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecICInboxTrans_P      : Transaction boite de reception IC
      //   - RecOutBoxPostReceipt_P : En-t^te reception boite d'envoi IC
      //   - RecInBoxPostReceipt_P  : En-t^te reception boite de reception IC
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      GetCompanyInfo;
      IF RecOutBoxPostReceipt_P."IC Partner Code" = CompanyInfo."IC Partner Code" THEN
        RecICPartner_L.GET(RecOutBoxPostReceipt_P."IC Partner Code")
      ELSE BEGIN
        RecICPartner_L.GET(RecOutBoxPostReceipt_P."IC Partner Code");
        CASE RecICPartner_L."Cereal Flow" OF
          RecICPartner_L."Cereal Flow"::Database :
            BEGIN
              RecICPartner_L.TESTFIELD("Cereal Inbox Details");
              RecICPartner_L.CHANGECOMPANY(RecICPartner_L."Cereal Inbox Details");
            END;
          RecICPartner_L."Cereal Flow"::"File Location" :
            RecICPartner_L.TESTFIELD("Use Company Datas");
          ELSE
            ERROR(Text8053009,RecICPartner_L.FIELDNAME("Cereal Flow"),RecICPartner_L."Cereal Flow");
        END;
        RecICPartner_L.GET(RecICInboxTrans_P."IC Partner Code");
      END;
      //Pas de test car le partenaire IC qui a initi' le flux n'est pas forc'ment fournisseur dans la soci't' de destination
      //IF (RecICPartner_L."Vendor No." = '') THEN
      //  ERROR(Text001,RecICPartner_L.TABLECAPTION,RecICPartner_L.Code,RecVendor_L.TABLECAPTION,
      //    RecOutBoxPostReceipt_P."IC Partner Code");

      RecInBoxPostReceipt_P.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecOutBoxPostReceipt_P);
      RecInBoxPostReceipt_P."IC Transaction No." := RecICInboxTrans_P."Transaction No.";
      RecInBoxPostReceipt_P."IC Partner Code" := RecICInboxTrans_P."IC Partner Code";
      RecInBoxPostReceipt_P."Transaction Source" := RecICInboxTrans_P."Transaction Source";
      IF NOT RecOutBoxPostReceipt_P."Shared Silo" AND
        (RecOutBoxPostReceipt_P."Receipt Type" <> RecOutBoxPostReceipt_P."Receipt Type"::Transfert)
      THEN BEGIN
        RecInBoxPostReceipt_P."Actual Location Code":= RecOutBoxPostReceipt_P."Location Code";
        RecICPartner_L.TESTFIELD("Set Off Location Code");
        RecInBoxPostReceipt_P."Location Code"       := RecICPartner_L."Set Off Location Code";
      END;
      RecInBoxPostReceipt_P."IC Source Document No.":= RecOutBoxPostReceipt_P."No.";
      IF RecInBoxPostReceipt_P."Receipt Type" = RecInBoxPostReceipt_P."Receipt Type"::Receipt THEN BEGIN
        RecInBoxPostReceipt_P."Collection Type" := RecInBoxPostReceipt_P."Collection Type"::Storage;
        RecInBoxPostReceipt_P."Purchase Date" := 0D;
      END;
      RecInBoxPostReceipt_P.INSERT;
    END;

    PROCEDURE OutboxDeliveryToInbox@8053006(VAR RecICInboxTrans_P@8053002 : Record 418;VAR RecOutboxPostedDelivery_P@8053000 : Record 8053338;VAR RecInboxPostedDelivery_P@8053001 : Record 8053340);
    VAR
      RecICPartner_L@8053003 : Record 413;
      RecVendor_L@8053004 : Record 23;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cr'e la livraison collecte dans la boOte de r'ception IC
      // Appel dans le rpt 513
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecICInboxTrans_P         : Transaction boOte de reception IC
      //   - RecOutboxPostedDelivery_P : livraison collecte de la boOte d'envoi IC
      //   - RecInboxPostedDelivery_P  : livraison collecte de la boOte de r'ception IC
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      GetCompanyInfo;
      IF RecOutboxPostedDelivery_P."IC Partner Code" = CompanyInfo."IC Partner Code" THEN
        RecICPartner_L.GET(RecOutboxPostedDelivery_P."IC Partner Code")
      ELSE BEGIN
        RecICPartner_L.GET(RecOutboxPostedDelivery_P."IC Partner Code");
        RecICPartner_L.TESTFIELD("Cereal Flow" ,RecICPartner_L."Cereal Flow"::Database);
        RecICPartner_L.TESTFIELD("Cereal Inbox Details");
        RecICPartner_L.CHANGECOMPANY(RecICPartner_L."Cereal Inbox Details");
        // Cas de la r'gule pour le compte de silo partag' soci't' de gestion et 2 soci't's utilisatrices
        IF RecICInboxTrans_P."On Behalf Of Partner-From Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
          RecICPartner_L.GET(RecICInboxTrans_P."On Behalf Of Partner-From Code");
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF RecICInboxTrans_P."On Behalf Of Partner-To Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            RecICPartner_L.GET(RecICInboxTrans_P."On Behalf Of Partner-To Code");
          END ELSE BEGIN
            RecICPartner_L.GET(RecICInboxTrans_P."IC Partner Code");
          END;
        END;
      END;

      //Todo cas des ventes ?
      //IF (RecICPartner_L."Vendor No." = '') THEN
      //  ERROR(Text001,RecICPartner_L.TABLECAPTION,RecICPartner_L.Code,RecVendor_L.TABLECAPTION,
      //    RecOutBoxPostReceipt_P."IC Partner Code");

      RecInboxPostedDelivery_P.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecOutboxPostedDelivery_P);
      RecInboxPostedDelivery_P."IC Transaction No." := RecICInboxTrans_P."Transaction No.";
      RecInboxPostedDelivery_P."IC Partner Code" := RecICInboxTrans_P."IC Partner Code";
      RecInboxPostedDelivery_P."Transaction Source" := RecICInboxTrans_P."Transaction Source";
      IF RecOutboxPostedDelivery_P."On Behalf Of Adjustment" THEN BEGIN
        RecICPartner_L.TESTFIELD(RecICPartner_L."Set Off Location Code");
        // Cas de la r'gule pour le compte de silo partag' soci't' de gestion et 2 soci't's utilisatrices
        IF RecICInboxTrans_P."On Behalf Of Partner-From Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
          RecInboxPostedDelivery_P."Location Code" := RecICPartner_L."Set Off Location Code";
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF RecICInboxTrans_P."On Behalf Of Partner-To Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            RecInboxPostedDelivery_P."Transfer-to Code" := RecICPartner_L."Set Off Location Code";
          END ELSE BEGIN
            // Cas du transfert de propri't' o- le code partenaire IC et le code partenaire IC origine sont identiques
            // -> Soci't' utilisatrice qui doit . la soci't' de gestion du silo partag'
            IF RecICInboxTrans_P."Ownership Transfer" AND
              (RecOutboxPostedDelivery_P."IC Partner Code" = RecOutboxPostedDelivery_P."IC Partner-From Code")
            THEN
              RecInboxPostedDelivery_P."Transfer-to Code" := RecICPartner_L."Set Off Location Code"
            ELSE
              RecInboxPostedDelivery_P."Location Code" := RecICPartner_L."Set Off Location Code";
          END;
        END;
      // --> 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF NOT RecOutboxPostedDelivery_P."Shared Silo" AND
          (RecOutboxPostedDelivery_P."Delivery Type" = RecOutboxPostedDelivery_P."Delivery Type"::Delivery)
        THEN BEGIN
          RecInboxPostedDelivery_P."Actual Location Code":= RecOutboxPostedDelivery_P."Location Code";
          RecICPartner_L.TESTFIELD("Set Off Location Code");
          RecInboxPostedDelivery_P."Location Code"       := RecICPartner_L."Set Off Location Code";
        END;
      // <-- 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
      END;
      RecInboxPostedDelivery_P."IC Source Document No." := RecOutboxPostedDelivery_P."No.";
      RecInboxPostedDelivery_P.INSERT;
    END;

    PROCEDURE OutBoxCollectLineToInBox@8053003(VAR RecInboxTrans_P@8053000 : Record 418;VAR RecOutboxPostRcptLine_P@8053001 : Record 8053292;VAR RecInBoxPostRcptline_P@8053002 : Record 8053294);

      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      //   - RecICInboxTrans_P       : Transaction boite de reception IC
      //   - RecOutboxPostRcptLine_P : Ligne reception boite d'envoi IC
      //   - RecInBoxPostRcptline_P  : Ligne reception  boite de reception IC
      // Sorties:
      //   -

      RecInBoxPostRcptline_P.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecOutboxPostRcptLine_P);

      RecInBoxPostRcptline_P."IC Partner Code"    := RecInboxTrans_P."IC Partner Code";
      RecInBoxPostRcptline_P.INSERT;
    END;

    PROCEDURE OutBoxCollectAnalysisToInBox@8053009(VAR RecInboxTrans_P@8053000 : Record 418;VAR RecOutBoxAnalysis_P@8053003 : Record 8053296;VAR RecInBoxAnalysis_P@8053001 : Record 8053298);
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cr'e la ligne analyse reception collecte IC dans la boOte de reception du partenaire
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecICInboxTrans_P       : Transaction boite de reception IC
      //   - RecOutBoxAnalysis_P : Analyses reception collecte boite d'envoi IC
      //   - RecInBoxAnalysis_P  : Analyses reception collecte boite de reception IC
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      RecInBoxAnalysis_P.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecOutBoxAnalysis_P);
      RecInBoxAnalysis_P."IC Transaction No." := RecInboxTrans_P."Transaction No.";
      RecInBoxAnalysis_P."Transaction Source" := RecInboxTrans_P."Transaction Source";
      RecInBoxAnalysis_P."IC Partner Code"    := RecInboxTrans_P."IC Partner Code";
      RecInBoxAnalysis_P.INSERT;
    END;
