    PROCEDURE CreateCollectReceiptDocument@1000000137(RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P@1000 : Record 8053290);
    VAR
      RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_L@8053000 : Record 8053290;
      RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L@8053006 : Record 8053294;
      RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L@8053007 : Record 8053298;
      RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L@8053008 : Record 8053302;
      RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L@8053012 : Record 8053291;
      RecHandedlInboxRcptLine_L@8053011 : Record 8053295;
      RecHDInBoxCollectAnalysis_L@8053010 : Record 8053299;
      RecHDInBoxCollectWeighing_L@8053009 : Record 8053303;
      RecCollectionRcptHeader_L@8053001 : Record 8052993;
      TmpCollectionRcptHeader_L@8053005 : TEMPORARY Record 8052993;
      RecCollectionReceiptLine_L@8053002 : Record 8053103;
      RecReceiptAnalysis_L@8053003 : Record 8052994;
      RecReceiptWeighing_L@8053004 : Record 8053094;
      TmpReceiptWeighing_L@8053026 : TEMPORARY Record 8053094;
      RecMcMasterDataRel_L@8053013 : Record 8053076;
      RecVendor_L@8053014 : Record 23;
      RecICDataCorrespondance_L@8053022 : Record 8053304;
      RecCellLocation_L@8053023 : Record 8053031;
      CduEngineMngt_L@8053018 : Codeunit 8061230;
      cduWeighingMgt_L@8053025 : Codeunit 8053010;
      cduAdvancedSetupManagement_L@8053016 : Codeunit 8061290;
      VendorCode_L@8053024 : Code[20];
      AnalysisCode_L@8053027 : Code[10];
      CduMCDataMgt_L@8053029 : Codeunit 8053306;
      DocNo_L@1000000000 : Code[20];
      RecShippingAgent_L@8053021 : Record 291;
      RecLocation_L@60000 : Record 14;
      RecCerealsChoice_L@1000000001 : Record 8053500;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cr'e la r'ception c'r'ales . partir de la reception c'r'ales enregistr'e de la boOte de reception IC
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P : En-t^te reception c'r'ale boOte de r'ception IC
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      GetCompanyInfo;
      CompanyInfo.TESTFIELD("IC Partner Code");

      WITH RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P DO BEGIN
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L.INIT;
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."No." := '';
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Receipt Type" :=  RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P."Receipt Type";
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L.INSERT(TRUE);
        TmpCollectionRcptHeader_L := RecCollectionRcptHeader_L ;
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P);
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L.VALIDATE("Shipment Date",RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P."Shipment Date");
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."No." := TmpCollectionRcptHeader_L."No." ;
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."No. Series" := TmpCollectionRcptHeader_L."No. Series" ;
        // --> 20150702-GEN15/001-TER01.02.01-1854-FBE01
        IF RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P."Receipt Type" = RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P."Receipt Type"::Resumption THEN
          RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Campaign No." := "Campaign No.";
        // <-- 20150702-GEN15/001-TER01.02.01-1854-FBE01
          // Validate de l'espece pour cr'er les lignes analyse
        // --> 20150612-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
        //BEFORE
        //RecCollectionRcptHeader_L.UpdateSpeciesAnalysis(RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P."Species Code");
        //RecCollectionRcptHeader_L.SuspendUpdateWeighingData(TRUE);
        //AFTER
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L.SuspendUpdateWeighingData(TRUE);
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Species Code" := GetSpeciesCorrespondence("IC Partner Code","Species Code",0);
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L.UpdateSpeciesAnalysis(RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Species Code");
        // <-- 20150612-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01

        // recherche de la 1ere cellule non control'e pour le magasin de compensation
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Cell Code" := RecCellLocation_L.GetUncontroledCell(RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Location Code");

        // recupere le code vendeur correspondant . l'agriculteur apporteur
        VendorCode_L := RecMcMasterDataRel_L.RetrieveCustVendFromThirdParty(
                                           RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Ship-to Contact No.",
                                           CduMCDataMgt_L.GetMCCompanyCode(''),1);
        IF VendorCode_L = '' THEN
          ERROR(Text8053000,RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Ship-to Contact No.",RecCollectionRcptHeader_L.TABLECAPTION);
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L.VALIDATE("Vendor No.",VendorCode_L);
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."IC Status" := RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."IC Status"::New;
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."IC Direction" := RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."IC Direction"::Incoming;
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."IC Partner Code" := RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P."IC Partner Code";
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."On Behalf Of"    := RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P."On Behalf Of";

        // --> Correspondance article
        // --> 20150612-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
        //BEFORE
        {
        IF RecICDataCorrespondance_L.GET(RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P."IC Partner Code",
                                         RecICDataCorrespondance_L."Flow Type"::CollectReceipt,
                                         RecICDataCorrespondance_L."Correspondance Type"::Item)
          THEN BEGIN
            ItemNo_L := CduEngineMngt_L.GetReferentialCorrespondance(RecICDataCorrespondance_L."Correspondence Template",
                                                         TRUE,RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P."Item No.",TRUE);
            IF ItemNo_L <> '' THEN BEGIN

              RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Item No." := ItemNo_L;
            END;
          END;
        }
        //AFTER
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Item No.":= GetItemCorrespondence("IC Partner Code","Item No.");
        // <-- 20150612-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
        // <-- Correspondance article
        // --> Correspondance Globale
        // --> 20150612-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
        //BEFORE
        {
        IF RecICDataCorrespondance_L.GET(RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P."IC Partner Code",
                                         RecICDataCorrespondance_L."Flow Type"::CollectReceipt,
                                         RecICDataCorrespondance_L."Correspondance Type"::Global)
          THEN BEGIN
            // --> Correspondance Vari't'
            VarietyCode_L := CduEngineMngt_L.GetCorresEmptyInclude(RecICDataCorrespondance_L."Correspondence Template",
                                          RecVariety_L.TABLENAME,RecVariety_L.FIELDNAME(RecVariety_L."Variety Code"),
                                          RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P."Variety Code",
                                          TRUE,TRUE);
            IF VarietyCode_L <> '' THEN
              RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Variety Code" := VarietyCode_L;
            // <-- Correspondance Vari't'
          END;
        }
        //AFTER
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Variety Code" := GetVarietyCorrespondence("IC Partner Code","Variety Code",0);
        // <-- 20150612-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
          IF RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Variety Code" = '' THEN
            IF cduAdvancedSetupManagement_L.GetAdvancedParameterValueBool('CER-RCPT-VARIETOBLIG') THEN
              RecCollectionRcptHeader_L.TESTFIELD("Variety Code");
        // <-- Correspondance Globale

        IF RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Seal No." = '' THEN
          RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Seal No." := '999999';

        // Controle des champs obligatoires et affectation par d'faut
        IF RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Vendor Delivery Note" = '' THEN
          IF cduAdvancedSetupManagement_L.GetAdvancedParameterValueBool('CER-RCPT-BLAGRIOBLIG') THEN
            RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Vendor Delivery Note" :=  '999999';

        IF RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Manual Weighing No." = '' THEN
          IF cduAdvancedSetupManagement_L.GetAdvancedParameterValueBool('CER-RCPT-TICKMAOBLIG') THEN
            RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Manual Weighing No." :=  RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P."No.";

        IF RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Shipment Date" = 0D THEN
          IF cduAdvancedSetupManagement_L.GetAdvancedParameterValueBool('CER-RCPT-DATLIVOBLIG') THEN
            RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Shipment Date" := RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Posting Date";

        IF RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Clean Containing" = RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Clean Containing"::" " THEN
          IF cduAdvancedSetupManagement_L.GetAdvancedParameterValueBool('CER-RCPT-CONTPROPRE')THEN
            RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Clean Containing" := RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Clean Containing"::No;

        // --> 20150206-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-2661-DRN01
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Shipping Agent Code" := GetShippingAgentCorrespondence("IC Partner Code","Shipping Agent Code");
        // <-- 20150206-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-2661-DRN01

        // --> 20150611-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-SDE01
        IF RecShippingAgent_L.GET(RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Shipping Agent Code") THEN
          RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Shipping Vendor Code":=RecShippingAgent_L."Vendor No.";
        // <-- 20150611-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-SDE01

        // --> 20150430-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-2943-FBE01
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Valid Receipt" := RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P."Valid Receipt";
        // <-- 20150430-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-2943-FBE01

        // --> 20150616-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-463-FBE01
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Trucks Registration" := RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P."Trucks Registration";
        IF RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Trucks Registration" =  '' THEN
          RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Trucks Registration" := '9999999999' ;
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Manual Voucher No." := RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P."Manual Voucher No.";
        IF RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Manual Voucher No." = '' THEN BEGIN
          GetCMCSetup;
          IF RecCMCSetup_G."Mandatory Manual Voucher No." THEN BEGIN
            RecLocation_L.GET(RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Location Code");
            IF NOT RecLocation_L."Computer Link Site/Siege" THEN
              RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Manual Voucher No." := '9999999999';
          END;
        END;
        // <-- 20150616-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-463-FBE01
        // --> 20150702-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-1854-FBE03
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Trailers Registration" := RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P."Trailers Registration";
        IF RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Trailers Registration" =  '' THEN
          RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Trailers Registration" := '9999999999' ;
        // <-- 20150702-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-1854-FBE03

        // <-- 20150624-CER170-TER01.01.00-448-FBE01
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L.Disinsectization := RecCollectionRcptHeader_L.Disinsectization::No;
        // --> 20150624-CER170-TER01.01.00-448-FBE01

        // --> 20240219-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3649-IJI01
        RecCMCSetup_G.GET;
        RecCerealsChoice_L.SETRANGE(Sales,TRUE);
        IF RecCerealsChoice_L.FINDFIRST THEN
          IF RecCMCSetup_G."Mandatory Farmer Choice" THEN
            IF RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Farmer Choice" = '' THEN
              RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Farmer Choice":= RecCerealsChoice_L.Code;
        // <-- 20240219-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3649-IJI01

        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L.MODIFY;
        RecCollectionRcptHeader_L.SuspendUpdateWeighingData(FALSE);

        // Creation reception valid'e dans la boite de reception g'r'e
        RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P);
        RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L."No." := TmpCollectionRcptHeader_L."No." ;
        RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L.INSERT;

        // Cr'ation des lignes d'analyse
        RecReceiptAnalysis_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Receipt Type","Receipt No.","Analysis Code");
        RecReceiptAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Receipt Type", RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Receipt Type");
        RecReceiptAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Receipt No.",TmpCollectionRcptHeader_L."No.");
        IF RecReceiptAnalysis_L.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          AnalysisCode_L := GetAnalysisCorrespondence("IC Partner Code",RecReceiptAnalysis_L."Analysis Code",1);
          IF AnalysisCode_L = '' THEN
            ERROR(Text8053001, RecReceiptAnalysis_L."Analysis Code")
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.GET("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source",AnalysisCode_L) THEN BEGIN
              RecReceiptAnalysis_L.Value := RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.Value;
              // --> 20150705-CER40-TER01.01.00-1887-SDE01
              RecReceiptAnalysis_L."Value to modify":=RecReceiptAnalysis_L.Value;
              // <-- 20150705-CER40-TER01.01.00-1887-SDE01
              RecReceiptAnalysis_L."Sort Order" := RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L."Sort Order";
              RecReceiptAnalysis_L.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        UNTIL RecReceiptAnalysis_L.NEXT = 0;

        // Cr'ation des lignes de reception
        RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.SETCURRENTKEY("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source","Line No.");
        RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.","IC Transaction No.");
        RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.SETRANGE("Transaction Source","Transaction Source");
        RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code","IC Partner Code");
        IF RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            RecCollectionReceiptLine_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L);
            RecCollectionReceiptLine_L."No." := TmpCollectionRcptHeader_L."No." ;
            // --> 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
            RecCollectionReceiptLine_L."Product Type" := RecCollectionReceiptLine_L."Product Type"::" ";
            // <-- 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
            // --> 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
            RecCollectionReceiptLine_L.VALIDATE(Quantity,RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.Quantity);
            // <-- 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
            RecCollectionReceiptLine_L.INSERT;
            RecHandedlInboxRcptLine_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L);
            RecHandedlInboxRcptLine_L."No." := TmpCollectionRcptHeader_L."No." ;
            RecHandedlInboxRcptLine_L.INSERT;
          UNTIL RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.NEXT = 0;

        // archive les analyses boite de reception
        RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.RESET;
        RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.SETCURRENTKEY("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source","Analysis Code");
        RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.","IC Transaction No.");
        RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Transaction Source","Transaction Source");
        RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code","IC Partner Code");
        IF RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            RecHDInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L);
            RecHDInBoxCollectAnalysis_L."Receipt No." := TmpCollectionRcptHeader_L."No." ;
            RecHDInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.INSERT;
          UNTIL RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.NEXT = 0;

        RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L.SETCURRENTKEY("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source");
        RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.","IC Transaction No.");
        RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L.SETRANGE("Transaction Source","Transaction Source");
        RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code","IC Partner Code");
        IF RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            cduWeighingMgt_L.CreateWeighingRec(RecReceiptWeighing_L);
            TmpReceiptWeighing_L := RecReceiptWeighing_L;
            RecReceiptWeighing_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L);
            RecReceiptWeighing_L."Source Document No." := TmpCollectionRcptHeader_L."No." ;
            RecReceiptWeighing_L."Entry No." := TmpReceiptWeighing_L."Entry No.";
            IF RecVendor_L.GET(RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Vendor No.") THEN BEGIN
              RecReceiptWeighing_L."Source No." := RecVendor_L."No.";
              RecReceiptWeighing_L.Name := RecVendor_L.Name;
              RecReceiptWeighing_L."Name 2" := RecVendor_L."Name 2";
              RecReceiptWeighing_L.Address:=RecVendor_L.Address;
              RecReceiptWeighing_L."Address 2" := RecVendor_L."Address 2";
              RecReceiptWeighing_L.City := RecVendor_L.City;
              RecReceiptWeighing_L."Post Code" := RecVendor_L."Post Code";
              RecReceiptWeighing_L.County := RecVendor_L.County;
              RecReceiptWeighing_L."Country/Region Code" := RecVendor_L."Country/Region Code";
            END;
            RecReceiptWeighing_L.MODIFY;
            RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Weighing No." := RecReceiptWeighing_L."Entry No.";
            RecCollectionRcptHeader_L.MODIFY;
            RecHDInBoxCollectWeighing_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L);
            RecHDInBoxCollectWeighing_L."Source Document No." := TmpCollectionRcptHeader_L."No." ;
            RecHDInBoxCollectWeighing_L.INSERT;
          UNTIL RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L.NEXT = 0;

        // --> 20141029-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-FBE01
        //InsertICDocumentMatching(ValidateReceipt(RecCollectionRcptHeader_L),RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L."Valid Receipt");
        // --> 20150723-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2002-DRN01
        // Si la cr'ation de IC Doc Matching n'est pas fait avant la validation, le recalcul du poinds au norme ne se fait pas
        // car le systSme s'appuie sur le principe du propri'taire pour le faire dans le cdu 8052985
        // ToDo . revoir car cela implique que le N› de r'ception valid'e = N› r'ception non valid'e
        InsertICDocumentMatching(RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."No.",RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L."Valid Receipt");
        // <-- 20150723-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2002-DRN01
        DocNo_L := ValidateReceipt(RecCollectionRcptHeader_L);
        // --> 20150723-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2002-DRN01
        //BEFORE
        //InsertICDocumentMatching(DocNo_L,RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L."Valid Receipt");
        // <-- 20150723-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2002-DRN01

        // si les analyses sont valides, on recalcule le poids aux normes dans la soci't' de destination
        // --> 20150703-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-1966-FBE02
        //IF RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L."Valid Receipt" AND NOT RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L."Shared Silo" THEN
        //  ReCalcReceiptWeight(DocNo_L,RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L."IC Partner Code",RecCollectionRcptHeader_L."Receipt Type");
        // <-- 20150703-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-1966-FBE02
        END;
        // <-- 20141029-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-FBE01
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateCollectReceiptDocument@1000000022(RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P@1000 : Record 8053290);
    VAR
      RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_L@8053000 : Record 8053290;
      RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L@8053006 : Record 8053294;
      RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L@8053007 : Record 8053298;
      RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L@8053008 : Record 8053302;
      RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L@8053012 : Record 8053291;
      RecHandedlInboxRcptLine_L@8053011 : Record 8053295;
      RecHDInBoxCollectAnalysis_L@8053010 : Record 8053299;
      RecHDInBoxCollectWeighing_L@8053009 : Record 8053303;
      RecPostedRcptHeader_L@8053001 : Record 8052995;
      RecPostedReceiptLine_L@8053002 : Record 8053104;
      RecPostedReceiptAnalysis_L@8053003 : Record 8052996;
      RecPostReceiptWeigh_L@8053004 : Record 8053095;
      RecMcMasterDataRel_L@8053013 : Record 8053076;
      RecVendor_L@8053014 : Record 23;
      RecICDataCorrespondance_L@8053022 : Record 8053304;
      RecVariety_L@8053020 : Record 8053070;
      RecItem_L@8053019 : Record 27;
      RecCellLocation_L@8053023 : Record 8053031;
      RecAnalysisCaract_L@8053028 : Record 8053092;
      RecIcDocMatching_L@1000000000 : Record 8053305;
      CduEngineMngt_L@8053018 : Codeunit 8061230;
      cduWeighingMgt_L@8053025 : Codeunit 8053010;
      cduAdvancedSetupManagement_L@8053016 : Codeunit 8061290;
      CduStatusManagement_L@1000000001 : Codeunit 8052985;
      ItemNo_L@8053017 : Code[20];
      VarietyCode_L@8053015 : Code[10];
      VendorCode_L@8053024 : Code[20];
      AnalysisCode_L@8053027 : Code[10];
      CduMCDataMgt_L@8053029 : Codeunit 8053306;
      RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L@1000000002 : Record 60135;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Modifie la r'ception c'r'ales enregistr'e . partir de la reception c'r'ales enregistr'e de la boOte de reception IC
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P : En-t^te reception c'r'ale boOte de r'ception IC
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      GetCompanyInfo;
      CompanyInfo.TESTFIELD("IC Partner Code");

      WITH RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P DO BEGIN
        RecIcDocMatching_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Source IC Partner","Source Type","Source Document Type","Source Document No.");
        RecIcDocMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source IC Partner",RecICInboxTrans_G."IC Partner Code");
        RecIcDocMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type");
        RecIcDocMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Document Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document Type");
        RecIcDocMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Document No.",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document No.");
        RecIcDocMatching_L.FINDFIRST();
        RecPostedRcptHeader_L.GET(RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P."Receipt Type",RecIcDocMatching_L."Destination Document No.");
        RecPostedRcptHeader_L.Status := RecPostedRcptHeader_L.Status :: Open;
        RecPostedRcptHeader_L.MODIFY;

        // recupere le code vendeur correspondant . l'agriculteur apporteur,
        // s'il est different de celui de la reception on valide le nouveau
        VendorCode_L := RecMcMasterDataRel_L.RetrieveCustVendFromThirdParty(
                                           RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P."Ship-to Contact No.",
                                           CduMCDataMgt_L.GetMCCompanyCode(''),1);
        IF VendorCode_L = '' THEN
          ERROR(Text8053000,"Ship-to Contact No.",RecPostedRcptHeader_L.TABLECAPTION);
        IF VendorCode_L <> RecPostedRcptHeader_L."Vendor No." THEN
          RecPostedRcptHeader_L.VALIDATE("Vendor No.",VendorCode_L);

        RecPostedRcptHeader_L."Transport Type" := "Transport Type";
        RecPostedRcptHeader_L."Trailers Registration" := "Trailers Registration";
        RecPostedRcptHeader_L."Weighing 1" := "Weighing 1";
        RecPostedRcptHeader_L."Weighing 2" := "Weighing 2";
        RecPostedRcptHeader_L."Net Weight" := "Net Weight";
        RecPostedRcptHeader_L."Standard Weight" := "Standard Weight";
        // --> 20160609-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-3065-NED
        RecPostedRcptHeader_L."Source Standard Weight"  := "Source Standard Weight";
        // <-- 20160609-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-3065-NED
        // --> 20150430-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-2943-FBE01
        RecPostedRcptHeader_L."Valid Receipt" := RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P."Valid Receipt";
        // <-- 20150430-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-2943-FBE01
        RecPostedRcptHeader_L.MODIFY;

        // Creation reception valid'e dans la boite de reception g'r'e
        RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P);
        RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L."No." := RecPostedRcptHeader_L."No." ;
        RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L.INSERT;

        // Modification des lignes de reception
        // --> 20160609-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-3065-NED
        //RecPostedRcptHeader_L."Source Standard Weight"  := "Source Standard Weight";
        // <-- 20160609-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-3065-NED
        RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.SETCURRENTKEY("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source","Line No.");
        RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.","IC Transaction No.");
        RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.SETRANGE("Transaction Source","Transaction Source");
        RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code","IC Partner Code");
        IF RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            IF NOT RecPostedReceiptLine_L.GET(RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L."Receipt Type", RecPostedRcptHeader_L."No." ,
                               RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L."Line No.") THEN BEGIN
              RecPostedReceiptLine_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L);
              RecPostedReceiptLine_L."No." := RecPostedRcptHeader_L."No." ;
              RecPostedReceiptLine_L."Collection Type" := RecPostedReceiptLine_L."Collection Type"::Storage;
              RecPostedReceiptLine_L."Item Ledger Entry No." := 0;
              RecPostedReceiptLine_L."Canceled Item Ledg. Entry No." := 0;
              RecPostedReceiptLine_L.INSERT;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              RecPostedReceiptLine_L.Quantity := RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.Quantity;
              RecPostedReceiptLine_L."Quantity To Affect" := RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L."Quantity To Affect";
              RecPostedReceiptLine_L.MODIFY;
            END;
            RecHandedlInboxRcptLine_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L);
            RecHandedlInboxRcptLine_L."No." := RecPostedRcptHeader_L."No." ;
            RecHandedlInboxRcptLine_L.INSERT;
          UNTIL RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.NEXT = 0;

        // Modification des lignes d'analyse
        RecPostedReceiptAnalysis_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Receipt Type","Receipt No.","Analysis Code");
        RecPostedReceiptAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Receipt Type", RecPostedRcptHeader_L."Receipt Type");
        RecPostedReceiptAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Receipt No.",RecPostedRcptHeader_L."No.");
        IF RecPostedReceiptAnalysis_L.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          AnalysisCode_L := GetAnalysisCorrespondence("IC Partner Code",RecPostedReceiptAnalysis_L."Analysis Code",1);
          IF AnalysisCode_L = '' THEN
            ERROR(Text8053001, RecPostedReceiptAnalysis_L."Analysis Code")
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.GET("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source",AnalysisCode_L) THEN BEGIN
              RecPostedReceiptAnalysis_L.Value := RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.Value;
              // --> 20150705-CER40-TER01.01.00-1887-SDE01
              RecPostedReceiptAnalysis_L."Value to modify":=RecPostedReceiptAnalysis_L.Value;
              // <-- 20150705-CER40-TER01.01.00-1887-SDE01
              RecPostedReceiptAnalysis_L.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        UNTIL RecPostedReceiptAnalysis_L.NEXT = 0;

        // archive les analyses boite de reception
        RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.RESET;
        RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.SETCURRENTKEY("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source","Analysis Code");
        RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.","IC Transaction No.");
        RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Transaction Source","Transaction Source");
        RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code","IC Partner Code");
        IF RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            RecHDInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L);
            RecHDInBoxCollectAnalysis_L."Receipt No." := RecPostedRcptHeader_L."No." ;
            RecHDInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.INSERT;
          UNTIL RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.NEXT = 0;

        // Modification des poids
        RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L.SETCURRENTKEY("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source");
        RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.","IC Transaction No.");
        RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L.SETRANGE("Transaction Source","Transaction Source");
        RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code","IC Partner Code");
        IF RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            // --> 20210521-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3555-CJY01
            {BEFORE
            RecPostReceiptWeigh_L.GET(RecPostedRcptHeader_L."Weighing No.");
            AFTER}
            IF RecPostReceiptWeigh_L.GET(RecPostedRcptHeader_L."Weighing No.") THEN BEGIN
            // <-- 20210521-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3555-CJY01

            IF VendorCode_L <> RecPostReceiptWeigh_L."Source No."  THEN BEGIN
              IF RecVendor_L.GET(VendorCode_L) THEN BEGIN
                RecPostReceiptWeigh_L."Source No." := RecVendor_L."No.";
                RecPostReceiptWeigh_L.Name := RecVendor_L.Name;
                RecPostReceiptWeigh_L."Name 2" := RecVendor_L."Name 2";
                RecPostReceiptWeigh_L.Address:=RecVendor_L.Address;
                RecPostReceiptWeigh_L."Address 2" := RecVendor_L."Address 2";
                RecPostReceiptWeigh_L.City := RecVendor_L.City;
                RecPostReceiptWeigh_L."Post Code" := RecVendor_L."Post Code";
                RecPostReceiptWeigh_L.County := RecVendor_L.County;
                RecPostReceiptWeigh_L."Country/Region Code" := RecVendor_L."Country/Region Code";
              END;
            END;
            RecPostReceiptWeigh_L."Weighing 1"     := RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L."Weighing 1";
            RecPostReceiptWeigh_L."Weighing 1 No." := RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L."Weighing 1 No.";
            RecPostReceiptWeigh_L."Weighing 2"     := RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L."Weighing 2";
            RecPostReceiptWeigh_L."Weighing 2 No." := RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L."Weighing 2 No.";
            RecPostReceiptWeigh_L."Net Weight"     := RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L."Net Weight";
            RecPostReceiptWeigh_L."Manual Weighing No."   := RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L."Manual Weighing No.";
            RecPostReceiptWeigh_L."Notebook Weighing No." := RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L."Notebook Weighing No.";
            RecPostReceiptWeigh_L.MODIFY;

            RecHDInBoxCollectWeighing_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L);
            RecHDInBoxCollectWeighing_L."Source Document No." := RecPostedRcptHeader_L."No." ;
            RecHDInBoxCollectWeighing_L.INSERT;
          // --> 20210521-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3555-CJY01
          END
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L.GET(RecPostedRcptHeader_L."Weighing No.") THEN BEGIN
              IF VendorCode_L <> RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L."Source No."  THEN BEGIN
                IF RecVendor_L.GET(VendorCode_L) THEN BEGIN
                  RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L."Source No." := RecVendor_L."No.";
                  RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L.Name := RecVendor_L.Name;
                  RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L."Name 2" := RecVendor_L."Name 2";
                  RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L.Address:=RecVendor_L.Address;
                  RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L."Address 2" := RecVendor_L."Address 2";
                  RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L.City := RecVendor_L.City;
                  RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L."Post Code" := RecVendor_L."Post Code";
                  RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L.County := RecVendor_L.County;
                  RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L."Country/Region Code" := RecVendor_L."Country/Region Code";
                END;
              END;
              RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L."Weighing 1"     := RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L."Weighing 1";
              RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L."Weighing 1 No." := RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L."Weighing 1 No.";
              RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L."Weighing 2"     := RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L."Weighing 2";
              RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L."Weighing 2 No." := RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L."Weighing 2 No.";
              RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L."Net Weight"     := RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L."Net Weight";
              RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L."Manual Weighing No."   := RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L."Manual Weighing No.";
              RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L."Notebook Weighing No." := RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L."Notebook Weighing No.";
              RecArchivedReceiptWeigh_L.MODIFY;

              RecHDInBoxCollectWeighing_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L);
              RecHDInBoxCollectWeighing_L."Source Document No." := RecPostedRcptHeader_L."No." ;
              RecHDInBoxCollectWeighing_L.INSERT;
            END;
          END;
          // <-- 20210521-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3555-CJY01

          UNTIL RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L.NEXT = 0;

        // --> 20150804-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-1991-DRN01
        //BEFORE
        {
        // --> 20150707-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-1966-FBE01
        CduStatusManagement_L.CallByICFunction(TRUE);
        // <-- 20150707-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-1966-FBE01
        CduStatusManagement_L.ReleasePostedReceipt(RecPostedRcptHeader_L);
        // --> 20150707-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-1966-FBE01
        CduStatusManagement_L.CallByICFunction(FALSE);
        // --> 20150707-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-1966-FBE01
        }
        // <-- 20150804-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-1991-DRN01

        IF RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L."Valid Receipt" THEN BEGIN
          RecIcDocMatching_L.Owner := RecIcDocMatching_L.Owner::Destination;
          RecIcDocMatching_L.MODIFY;
          // --> 20150703-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-1966-FBE02
          //IF NOT RecPostedRcptHeader_L."Shared Silo" THEN
          //  ReCalcReceiptWeight(RecPostedRcptHeader_L."No.","IC Partner Code",RecPostedRcptHeader_L."Receipt Type");
          // --> 20150521-CER143-TER01.01.00-359-FBE01
          //MakeCollectDistribute(RecPostedRcptHeader_L);
          // <-- 20150521-CER143-TER01.01.00-359-FBE01
          // <-- 20150703-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-1966-FBE02

          // --> 20150804-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-1991-DRN01
          ReCalcReceiptWeight(RecPostedRcptHeader_L."No.","IC Partner Code",RecPostedRcptHeader_L."Receipt Type");
          MakeCollectDistribute(RecPostedRcptHeader_L);
          // <-- 20150804-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-1991-DRN01
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateDeliveryDoc@1000000014(RecInboxPostedDelivery_P@8053000 : Record 8053340);
    VAR
      RecDelivery_L@8053001 : Record 8053013;
      RecCMCSetup_L@8053003 : Record 8052982;
      RecCellLocation_L@8053004 : Record 8053031;
      RecInboxPostedDelAnal_L@8053002 : Record 8053344;
      RecDeliveryAnalysis_L@8053005 : Record 8053030;
      RecInboxPostWeighing_L@8053007 : Record 8053302;
      RecWeighing_L@8053008 : Record 8053094;
      PostedDeliveryNo_L@8053011 : Code[20];
      RecMcMasterDataRel_L@1000000004 : Record 8053076;
      CduMCDataMgt_L@1000000003 : Codeunit 8053306;
      RecIcPartner_L@1000000002 : Record 413;
      CustomerCode_L@1000000001 : Code[20];
      ThirdPartyCode_L@1000000000 : Code[20];
      AnalysisCode_L@1000000006 : Code[10];
      AllMandatoryValueOk_L@1000000005 : Boolean;
      RecShippingOrder_L@8053006 : Record 8053265;
      RecShippingAgent_L@8053009 : Record 291;
      RecLocation_L@60000 : Record 14;
      RecSalesContract_L@1000000007 : Record 8053012;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cr'e et valide la livraison collecte . partir de la boOte de r'ception IC
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecInboxPostedDelivery_P : livraison Collecte de la boOte de r'ception IC
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      WITH RecInboxPostedDelivery_P DO BEGIN
        RecDelivery_L.INIT;
        RecDelivery_L."Delivery Type" := RecDelivery_L."Delivery Type"::Delivery;
        RecDelivery_L."IC Direction" := RecDelivery_L."IC Direction"::Incoming;
        RecDelivery_L.INSERT(TRUE);
        RecDelivery_L."Posting Date" := "Posting Date";
        // --> 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        RecDelivery_L."Actual Location Code" := "Actual Location Code";
        //RecDelivery_L."Sales Date" := "Sales Date";
        // --> 20161007-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2393-NED
        RecDelivery_L."Campaign No." := "Campaign No.";
        // <-- 20161007-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2393-NED
        RecDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Sales Date","Sales Date");
        // <-- 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        RecDelivery_L."Requested Delivery Date" := "Requested Delivery Date";
        RecDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Location Code","Location Code");
        RecDelivery_L."Campaign No." := "Campaign No.";
        // recupere le tiers MS correspondant au code client dans la diffusion des tiers MS pour la soci't' d'origine
        ThirdPartyCode_L := RecMcMasterDataRel_L.RetrieveThirdPartyFromCustVend(
                                 "Customer No.",RecIcPartner_L.GetMcCompanyCode("IC Partner Code"),0);
        // recupere le code client correspondant au tiers MS dans la diffusion des tiers MS pour la soci't' destinataire
        CustomerCode_L := RecMcMasterDataRel_L.RetrieveCustVendFromThirdParty(
                                         ThirdPartyCode_L, CduMCDataMgt_L.GetMCCompanyCode(''),0);
        IF CustomerCode_L = '' THEN
          ERROR(Text8053006,"Customer No.",RecDelivery_L.TABLECAPTION) ;
        RecDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Customer No.",CustomerCode_L);
        RecDelivery_L."Ship-to Code"      := "Ship-to Code";
        RecDelivery_L."Ship-to Name"      := "Ship-to Name";
        RecDelivery_L."Ship-to Name 2"    := "Ship-to Name 2";
        RecDelivery_L."Ship-to Address"   := "Ship-to Address";
        RecDelivery_L."Ship-to Address 2" := "Ship-to Address 2";
        RecDelivery_L."Ship-to City"      := "Ship-to City";
        RecDelivery_L."Ship-to Contact"   := "Ship-to Contact";
        RecDelivery_L."Ship-to Post Code" := "Ship-to Post Code";
        RecDelivery_L."Ship-to County"    := "Ship-to County" ;
        RecDelivery_L."Ship-to Country/Region Code" := "Ship-to Country/Region Code";

        RecDelivery_L."IC Partner Code" := "IC Partner Code";
        // <-- 20150612-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
        //RecDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Species Code","Species Code");
        RecDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Species Code" , GetSpeciesCorrespondence("IC Partner Code","Species Code",0));
        // <-- 20150612-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
        RecDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Item No.",GetItemCorrespondence("IC Partner Code","Item No."));
        RecDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Cell Code",RecCellLocation_L.GetUncontroledCell("Location Code"));
        RecDelivery_L."IC Source Document No." := "No.";
        RecDelivery_L."Without Sales Contract" := "Without Sales Contract";
        RecDelivery_L."Contract No.":= "Contract No.";
        // --> 20150805-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-1834-DRN01
        IF RecSalesContract_L.GET("Contract No.") THEN BEGIN
          RecDelivery_L."Weight Standards Already" := RecSalesContract_L."Standard Weight Deliveries";
          RecDelivery_L."Unit Price" := RecSalesContract_L."Unit Price";
          // --> 20150904-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2205-FBE01
        //END;
          RecDelivery_L."Product Type" := RecSalesContract_L."Invoice Type";
        END ELSE
          RecDelivery_L."Product Type"     := "Product Type";
          // <-- 20150904-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2205-FBE01

        // <-- 20150805-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-1834-DRN01
        //RecDelivery_L."Manual Transfer No." := "Manual Transfer No.";
        RecDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Trucker Name","Trucker Name");
        RecDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Trailers Registration No.","Trailers Registration No.");
        RecDelivery_L.Area := Area; // MAJ par le validate de customer No
        RecDelivery_L."Transport Method" := "Transport Method";
        // --> 20150904-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2205-FBE01
        //RecDelivery_L."Product Type"     := "Product Type";
        // <-- 20150904-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2205-FBE01
        RecDelivery_L."Transaction Specification" := "Transaction Specification";
        RecDelivery_L."Active Subtance Used"  := "Active Subtance Used";
        RecDelivery_L."Clean Containing"      := "Clean Containing";
        RecDelivery_L."Pre-Stockage Duration" := "Pre-Stockage Duration";

        RecDelivery_L."Transaction Type"  := "Transaction Type";
        RecDelivery_L."Exit Point"        := "Exit Point";
        RecDelivery_L."Shipping Type"     := "Shipping Type";
        // --> 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        {Before
        // --> 20150206-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-2661-DRN01
        //RecDelivery_L."Shipping Agent Code" := GetShippingAgentCorrespondence("IC Partner Code","Shipping Agent Code");
        // <-- 20150206-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-2661-DRN01
        After}
        RecDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Shipping Agent Code",GetShippingAgentCorrespondence("IC Partner Code","Shipping Agent Code"));
        // <-- 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        // --> 20150611-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-SDE01
        IF RecShippingAgent_L.GET(RecDelivery_L."Shipping Agent Code") THEN
          RecDelivery_L."Shipping Vendor Code":=RecShippingAgent_L."Vendor No.";

        RecDelivery_L."Shipping Order No." := GetICDocumentMatching("Shipping Order No.",1,"IC Partner Code",
                               RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type"::"Shipping Document");

        IF (RecDelivery_L."Shipping Order No."<>'') THEN BEGIN
          IF RecShippingOrder_L.GET(RecDelivery_L."Shipping Order No.") AND (RecShippingOrder_L."Charter No"<>'')
          // --> 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
          //THEN RecDelivery_L."Charter No." := RecShippingOrder_L."Charter No";
          THEN BEGIN
            RecDelivery_L."Charter No." := RecShippingOrder_L."Charter No";
            RecDelivery_L."Grid Type"   := RecShippingOrder_L."Grid Type";
            RecDelivery_L."Area Code"   := RecShippingOrder_L."Area code";
            RecDelivery_L."Transport Unit Cost" := RecShippingOrder_L."Transport Unit Cost";
            RecDelivery_L.Km := RecShippingOrder_L.Km;
            RecDelivery_L."Transport Amount" := RecShippingOrder_L."Transport Amount";
          END;
          // <-- 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        END;


        // <-- 20150611-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-SDE01
        // --> 20150616-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-463-FBE01
        RecDelivery_L."Trucks Registration" :=  "Trucks Registration";
        IF RecDelivery_L."Trucks Registration" =  '' THEN
          RecDelivery_L."Trucks Registration" := '9999999999' ;
        RecDelivery_L."Manual Voucher No." := "Manual Voucher No.";
        IF RecDelivery_L."Manual Voucher No." = '' THEN BEGIN
          GetCMCSetup;
          IF RecCMCSetup_G."Mandatory Manual Voucher No." THEN BEGIN
            RecLocation_L.GET("Location Code");
            IF NOT RecLocation_L."Computer Link Site/Siege" THEN
              RecDelivery_L."Manual Voucher No." := '9999999999';
          END;
        END;
        // <-- 20150616-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-463-FBE01
        // --> 20150702-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-1854-FBE03
        RecDelivery_L."Trailers Registration No." := "Trailers Registration No.";
        IF RecDelivery_L."Trailers Registration No." =  '' THEN
          RecDelivery_L."Trailers Registration No." := '9999999999' ;
        // <-- 20150702-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-1854-FBE03

        RecDelivery_L."Terminated Control" := "Terminated Control";
        RecDelivery_L."Loading Method"     := "Loading Method";
        RecDelivery_L."Missing Cleaning Certificate" := "Missing Cleaning Certificate";
        RecDelivery_L."Conformity Containing" := "Conformity Containing";
        //RecDelivery_L."Conformity Reason"  := "Conformity Reason";
        RecDelivery_L.Consignment := Consignment;
        RecDelivery_L.Application := Application;

        // <-- 20150624-CER170-TER01.01.00-448-FBE01
        RecDelivery_L.Disinsectization := RecDelivery_L.Disinsectization::No;
        // --> 20150624-CER170-TER01.01.00-448-FBE01

        //D'sinsectisation
        // --> 20150702-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-1854-FBE04
        //RecDelivery_L."Disinsectization Output"        := "Disinsectization Output";
        RecDelivery_L."Disinsectization Output"        := RecDelivery_L."Disinsectization Output"::" ";
        //RecDelivery_L."Disinsectization Code Output"   := "Disinsectization Code Output";
        //RecDelivery_L."Disinsectization Dosage Output" := "Disinsectization Dosage Output";
        //RecDelivery_L."Disinsectization Period Output" := "Disinsectization Period Output";
        //RecDelivery_L."Pump Code"                      := "Pump Code";
        // <-- 20150702-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-1854-FBE04
        //ToDo Derniers chargements ?
        // --> 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
        RecDelivery_L."On Behalf Of" :=  "On Behalf Of";
        RecDelivery_L."Actual Location Code" := "Actual Location Code";
        // <-- 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE

        //Cr'ation des analyses
        AllMandatoryValueOk_L := TRUE;
        RecDeliveryAnalysis_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Delivery Type","Delivery No.","Analysis Code");
        RecDeliveryAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Delivery Type", RecDelivery_L."Delivery Type");
        RecDeliveryAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Delivery No.",RecDelivery_L."No.");
        IF RecDeliveryAnalysis_L.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          AnalysisCode_L := GetAnalysisCorrespondence("IC Partner Code",RecDeliveryAnalysis_L."Analysis Code",1);
          IF AnalysisCode_L = '' THEN
            ERROR(Text8053001, RecDeliveryAnalysis_L."Analysis Code")
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF RecInboxPostedDelAnal_L.GET("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source",
                     RecInboxPostedDelAnal_L.Type,AnalysisCode_L) THEN BEGIN
              RecDeliveryAnalysis_L.Value := RecInboxPostedDelAnal_L.Value;
              RecDeliveryAnalysis_L."Sort Order" := RecInboxPostedDelAnal_L."Sort Order";
              RecDeliveryAnalysis_L.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        UNTIL RecDeliveryAnalysis_L.NEXT = 0;

        // Cr'ation de la pes'e
        // L'enregistrement de pes'e est cr'' lors du validate de l'article par la fct UpdateWeighingDataFromDelivery du cdu
        // CMC Weighing Management
        RecDelivery_L."Weighing 1" := "Weighing 1";
        RecDelivery_L."Weighing 1 No." := "Weighing 1 No.";
        RecDelivery_L."Weighing 2" := "Weighing 2";
        RecDelivery_L."Weighing 2 No." := "Weighing 2 No.";
        // --> 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        //RecDelivery_L."Net Weight" := "Net Weight";
        RecDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Net Weight","Net Weight");
        // <-- 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        RecDelivery_L."Standard Weight" := "Standard Weight";
        RecDelivery_L."Scale Weight" := "Scale Weight";
        // --> 20150430-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-2944-FBE01
        // --> 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
        //IF RecInboxPostedDelivery_P."Shared Silo" THEN
        // <-- 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
          RecDelivery_L."Source Standard Weight" := "Standard Weight";
        // <-- 20150430-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-2944-FBE01
        // On r'cupSre de la boOte de reception IC les informations de pes'e d'origine (Poids, DSD, ID pont bascule, N› ticket bascule)
        IF RecInboxPostWeighing_L.GET("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source") THEN BEGIN
          RecWeighing_L.GET(RecDelivery_L."Weighing No.");
          RecWeighing_L."Weighing 1" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Weighing 1";
          RecWeighing_L."Weighing 1 No." := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Weighing 1 No.";
          RecWeighing_L."Weighing 2" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Weighing 2";
          RecWeighing_L."Weighing 2 No." := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Weighing 2 No.";
          RecWeighing_L."Net Weight" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Net Weight";
          RecWeighing_L."Toggle Bridge Code W1" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Toggle Bridge Code W1";
          RecWeighing_L."Toggle Bridge Code W2" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Toggle Bridge Code W2";
          RecWeighing_L."Weight 1 Manual Update" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Weight 1 Manual Update";
          RecWeighing_L."Weight 1 Manual Entry" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Weight 1 Manual Entry";
          RecWeighing_L."Weight 2 Manual Update" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Weight 2 Manual Update";
          RecWeighing_L."Weight 2 Manual Entry" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Weight 2 Manual Entry";
          RecWeighing_L."Manual Weighing No." := "Manual Weighing No.";
          RecWeighing_L."Notebook Weighing No." := "Notebook Weighing No.";
          RecWeighing_L.Name         := "Ship-to Name";
          RecWeighing_L."Name 2"     := "Ship-to Name 2";
          RecWeighing_L.Address      := "Ship-to Address";
          RecWeighing_L."Address 2"  := "Ship-to Address 2";
          RecWeighing_L.City         := "Ship-to City";
          RecWeighing_L."Post Code"  := "Ship-to Post Code";
          RecWeighing_L.County       := "Ship-to County" ;
          RecWeighing_L."Country/Region Code" := "Ship-to Country/Region Code";
          RecWeighing_L.MODIFY;
          RecDelivery_L."Manual Weighing No." := RecWeighing_L."Manual Weighing No.";
          RecDelivery_L."Notebook Weighing No." := RecWeighing_L."Notebook Weighing No.";
        END;

        // <-- 20200629-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3484-SCR01
        RecDelivery_L."Seal No." := "Seal No.";
        // --> 20200629-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3484-SCR01

        RecDelivery_L.MODIFY;

        //Lancement de la validation
        PostedDeliveryNo_L := ValidateDelivery(RecDelivery_L);

        //Archivage de la livraison transfert collecte IC
        ArchiveDeliveryInbox(RecInboxPostedDelivery_P,PostedDeliveryNo_L);

        //Insertion dans la table des correspondances document IC
        InsertICDocumentMatching(PostedDeliveryNo_L,AllMandatoryValueOk_L);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateDeliveryDoc@8053024(RecInboxPostedDelivery_P@8053000 : Record 8053340);
    VAR
      RecPostedDelivery_L@8053001 : Record 8053014;
      RecCMCSetup_L@8053003 : Record 8052982;
      RecCellLocation_L@8053004 : Record 8053031;
      RecInboxPostedDelAnal_L@8053002 : Record 8053344;
      RecDeliveryAnalysis_L@8053005 : Record 8053030;
      RecInboxPostWeighing_L@8053007 : Record 8053302;
      RecWeighing_L@8053008 : Record 8053094;
      PostedDeliveryNo_L@8053011 : Code[20];
      RecMcMasterDataRel_L@1000000004 : Record 8053076;
      CduMCDataMgt_L@1000000003 : Codeunit 8053306;
      RecIcPartner_L@1000000002 : Record 413;
      CustomerCode_L@1000000001 : Code[20];
      ThirdPartyCode_L@1000000000 : Code[20];
      AnalysisCode_L@1000000006 : Code[10];
      AllMandatoryValueOk_L@1000000005 : Boolean;
      RecIcDocMatching_L@8053006 : Record 8053305;
      CduSalesStatusMgt_L@8053009 : Codeunit 8052987;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Modifie la livraison collecte . partir de la boOte de r'ception IC
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecInboxPostedDelivery_P : livraison Collecte de la boOte de r'ception IC
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      WITH RecInboxPostedDelivery_P DO BEGIN
        RecIcDocMatching_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Destination IC Partner","Destination Type","Destination Document Type",
                                          "Destination Document No.");
        RecIcDocMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination IC Partner",RecICInboxTrans_G."IC Partner Code");
        RecIcDocMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type");
        RecIcDocMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination Document Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document Type");
        RecIcDocMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination Document No.",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document No.");
        RecIcDocMatching_L.FINDFIRST();
        RecPostedDelivery_L.GET(RecInboxPostedDelivery_P."Delivery Type",RecIcDocMatching_L."Source Document No.");
        RecPostedDelivery_L.Status := RecPostedDelivery_L.Status :: Open;
        RecPostedDelivery_L.MODIFY;

        RecPostedDelivery_L."Source Standard Weight" := RecInboxPostedDelivery_P."Source Standard Weight";
        RecPostedDelivery_L."Invoicing Weight"       := RecInboxPostedDelivery_P."Invoicing Weight";

        //Archivage de la livraison transfert collecte IC
        ArchiveDeliveryInbox(RecInboxPostedDelivery_P,PostedDeliveryNo_L);

        //Lancement de la validation
        CduSalesStatusMgt_L.ReleasePostedDelivery(RecPostedDelivery_L);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateTransferDeliveryDoc@8053010(RecInboxPostedDelivery_P@8053000 : Record 8053340);
    VAR
      RecTransferDelivery_L@8053001 : Record 8053013;
      RecCMCSetup_L@8053003 : Record 8052982;
      RecCellLocation_L@8053004 : Record 8053031;
      RecInboxPostedDelAnal_L@8053002 : Record 8053344;
      RecTransferDeliveryAnalysis_L@8053005 : Record 8053030;
      RecInboxPostWeighing_L@8053007 : Record 8053302;
      RecWeighing_L@8053008 : Record 8053094;
      AnalysisCode_L@8053006 : Code[10];
      PostedTransferDeliveryNo_L@8053011 : Code[20];
      AllMandatoryValueOk_L@1000000000 : Boolean;
      RecShippingAgent_L@8053009 : Record 291;
      RecShippingOrder_L@8053010 : Record 8053265;
      RecLocation_L@60000 : Record 14;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cr'e et valide la livraison transfert collecte . partir de la boOte de r'ception IC
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecInboxPostedDelivery_P : livraison transfert collecte de la boOte de r'ception IC
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      WITH RecInboxPostedDelivery_P DO BEGIN
        RecTransferDelivery_L.INIT;
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Delivery Type" := RecTransferDelivery_L."Delivery Type"::Transfert;
        RecTransferDelivery_L."IC Direction" := RecTransferDelivery_L."IC Direction"::Incoming;
        RecTransferDelivery_L.INSERT(TRUE);
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Posting Date" := "Posting Date";
        // --> 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        RecTransferDelivery_L."On Behalf Of Adjustment" := "On Behalf Of Adjustment";
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Actual Location Code" := "Actual Location Code";
        //RecTransferDelivery_L."Sales Date" := "Sales Date";
        RecTransferDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Sales Date","Sales Date");
        // <-- 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Requested Delivery Date" := "Requested Delivery Date";
        RecTransferDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Location Code","Location Code");
        RecTransferDelivery_L."IC Partner Code" := "IC Partner Code";
        // --> 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        //RecTransferDelivery_L."On Behalf Of Adjustment" := "On Behalf Of Adjustment";
        // <-- 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        // Cas de la r'gule pour le compte de silo partag' soci't' de gestion et 2 soci't's utilisatrices
        RecTransferDelivery_L."IC Partner-From Code" := "IC Partner-From Code";
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Ownership Transfer" := "Ownership Transfer";
        IF NOT "Ownership Transfer" THEN BEGIN
          RecTransferDelivery_L."Independent Transfer" := TRUE;
          RecCMCSetup_L.GET;
          RecCMCSetup_L.TESTFIELD("Indep. Transfer Transit Code");
          RecTransferDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Indep. Transfer Transit Code",RecCMCSetup_L."Indep. Transfer Transit Code");
        END;
        RecTransferDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Transfer-to Code","Transfer-to Code");
        //ToDo Adresse de livraison + synchro avec pes'e
        // --> 20150702-GEN15/001-TER01.02.01-1854-FBE01
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Campaign No." := "Campaign No.";
        // <-- 20150702-GEN15/001-TER01.02.01-1854-FBE01
        RecTransferDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Cell Code",RecCellLocation_L.GetUncontroledCell("Location Code"));
        // <-- 20150612-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
        //RecTransferDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Species Code","Species Code");
        RecTransferDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Species Code" , GetSpeciesCorrespondence("IC Partner Code","Species Code",0));
        // <-- 20150612-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
        RecTransferDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Item No.",GetItemCorrespondence("IC Partner Code","Item No."));
        RecTransferDelivery_L."IC Source Document No." := "No.";
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Manual Transfer No." := "Manual Transfer No.";
        RecTransferDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Trucker Name","Trucker Name");
        RecTransferDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Trailers Registration No.","Trailers Registration No.");
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Transport Method" := "Transport Method";
        RecTransferDelivery_L.Area := Area;
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Active Subtance Used" := "Active Subtance Used";
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Clean Containing" := "Clean Containing";
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Pre-Stockage Duration" := "Pre-Stockage Duration";
        // --> 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        {Before
        // --> 20150206-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-2661-DRN01
        // RecTransferDelivery_L."Shipping Agent Code" := GetShippingAgentCorrespondence("IC Partner Code","Shipping Agent Code");
        // <-- 20150206-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-2661-DRN01
        After}
        RecTransferDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Shipping Agent Code",GetShippingAgentCorrespondence("IC Partner Code","Shipping Agent Code"));
        // <-- 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED

        // --> 20150611-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-SDE01
        IF RecShippingAgent_L.GET(RecTransferDelivery_L."Shipping Agent Code") THEN
          RecTransferDelivery_L."Shipping Vendor Code":=RecShippingAgent_L."Vendor No.";

        RecTransferDelivery_L."Shipping Order No." := GetICDocumentMatching("Shipping Order No.",1,"IC Partner Code",
                               RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type"::"Shipping Document");

        IF (RecTransferDelivery_L."Shipping Order No."<>'') THEN BEGIN
          IF RecShippingOrder_L.GET(RecTransferDelivery_L."Shipping Order No.") AND (RecShippingOrder_L."Charter No"<>'')
          // --> 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
          //THEN RecTransferDelivery_L."Charter No.":=RecShippingOrder_L."Charter No";
          THEN BEGIN
            RecTransferDelivery_L."Charter No.":=RecShippingOrder_L."Charter No";
            RecTransferDelivery_L."Grid Type" := RecShippingOrder_L."Grid Type";
            RecTransferDelivery_L."Area Code" := RecShippingOrder_L."Area code";
            RecTransferDelivery_L."Transport Unit Cost" := RecShippingOrder_L."Transport Unit Cost";
            RecTransferDelivery_L.Km := RecShippingOrder_L.Km;
            RecTransferDelivery_L."Transport Amount" := RecShippingOrder_L."Transport Amount";
          END;
          // <-- 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        END;

        // <-- 20150611-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-SDE01
        // --> 20150616-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-463-FBE01
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Trucks Registration" :=  "Trucks Registration";
        IF RecTransferDelivery_L."Trucks Registration" =  '' THEN BEGIN
          GetCMCSetup;
          IF NOT RecCMCSetup_G."Registration Not Mandatory T.R" THEN
            RecTransferDelivery_L."Trucks Registration" := '9999999999' ;
        END;
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Manual Voucher No." := "Manual Voucher No.";
        IF RecTransferDelivery_L."Manual Voucher No." = '' THEN BEGIN
          GetCMCSetup;
          IF RecCMCSetup_G."Mandatory Manual Voucher No." THEN BEGIN
            RecLocation_L.GET("Location Code");
            IF NOT RecLocation_L."Computer Link Site/Siege" THEN
              RecTransferDelivery_L."Manual Voucher No." := '9999999999';
          END;
        END;
        // <-- 20150616-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-463-FBE01
        // --> 20150702-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-1854-FBE03
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Trailers Registration No." := "Trailers Registration No.";
        IF RecTransferDelivery_L."Trailers Registration No." =  '' THEN
          RecTransferDelivery_L."Trailers Registration No." := '9999999999' ;
        // <-- 20150702-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-1854-FBE03
        // -->  20150617-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE02
        IF "Ownership Transfer" THEN BEGIN
          RecTransferDelivery_L."Clean Containing" := RecTransferDelivery_L."Clean Containing"::Yes;
          RecTransferDelivery_L."Conformity Containing" := RecTransferDelivery_L."Conformity Containing"::Yes;
          RecTransferDelivery_L."Terminated Control" := TRUE
        END;
        // <-- 20150617-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE02

        // <-- 20150624-CER170-TER01.01.00-448-FBE01
        RecTransferDelivery_L.Disinsectization := RecTransferDelivery_L.Disinsectization::No;
        // --> 20150624-CER170-TER01.01.00-448-FBE01

        //D'sinsectisation
        // --> 20150702-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-1854-FBE04
        //RecTransferDelivery_L."Disinsectization Output" := "Disinsectization Output";
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Disinsectization Output" := RecTransferDelivery_L."Disinsectization Output"::" ";
        //RecTransferDelivery_L."Disinsectization Code Output" := "Disinsectization Code Output";
        //RecTransferDelivery_L."Disinsectization Dosage Output" := "Disinsectization Dosage Output";
        //RecTransferDelivery_L."Disinsectization Period Output" := "Disinsectization Period Output";
        //RecTransferDelivery_L."Pump Code" := "Pump Code";
        // <-- 20150702-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-1854-FBE04
        //ToDo Derniers chargements ?

        RecTransferDelivery_L."Conformity Containing" := "Conformity Containing";
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Loading Method" := "Loading Method";
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Terminated Control" := "Terminated Control";
        //Cr'ation des analyses
        AllMandatoryValueOk_L := FALSE;
        RecTransferDeliveryAnalysis_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Delivery Type","Delivery No.","Analysis Code");
        RecTransferDeliveryAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Delivery Type", RecTransferDelivery_L."Delivery Type");
        RecTransferDeliveryAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Delivery No.",RecTransferDelivery_L."No.");
        IF RecTransferDeliveryAnalysis_L.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          AnalysisCode_L := GetAnalysisCorrespondence("IC Partner Code",RecTransferDeliveryAnalysis_L."Analysis Code",1);
          IF AnalysisCode_L = '' THEN
            ERROR(Text8053001, RecTransferDeliveryAnalysis_L."Analysis Code")
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF RecInboxPostedDelAnal_L.GET("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source",
                     RecInboxPostedDelAnal_L.Type,AnalysisCode_L) THEN BEGIN
              RecTransferDeliveryAnalysis_L.Value := RecInboxPostedDelAnal_L.Value;
              RecTransferDeliveryAnalysis_L."Sort Order" := RecInboxPostedDelAnal_L."Sort Order";
              RecTransferDeliveryAnalysis_L.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        UNTIL RecTransferDeliveryAnalysis_L.NEXT = 0;

        // Cr'ation de la pes'e
        // L'enregistrement de pes'e est cr'' lors du validate de l'article par la fct UpdateWeighingDataFromDelivery du cdu
        // CMC Weighing Management
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Weighing 1" := "Weighing 1";
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Weighing 1 No." := "Weighing 1 No.";
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Weighing 2" := "Weighing 2";
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Weighing 2 No." := "Weighing 2 No.";
        // --> 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        //RecTransferDelivery_L."Net Weight" := "Net Weight";
        RecTransferDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Net Weight","Net Weight");
        // <-- 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Standard Weight" := "Standard Weight";
        RecTransferDelivery_L."Scale Weight" := "Scale Weight";
        // On r'cupSre de la boOte de reception IC les informations de pes'e d'origine (Poids, DSD, ID pont bascule, N› ticket bascule)
        //Cas du transfert de propri't' -> pas d'enregistrement de pes'e
        IF RecInboxPostWeighing_L.GET("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source") THEN BEGIN
          RecWeighing_L.GET(RecTransferDelivery_L."Weighing No.");
          RecWeighing_L."Weighing 1" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Weighing 1";
          RecWeighing_L."Weighing 1 No." := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Weighing 1 No.";
          RecWeighing_L."Weighing 2" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Weighing 2";
          RecWeighing_L."Weighing 2 No." := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Weighing 2 No.";
          RecWeighing_L."Net Weight" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Net Weight";
          RecWeighing_L."Toggle Bridge Code W1" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Toggle Bridge Code W1";
          RecWeighing_L."Toggle Bridge Code W2" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Toggle Bridge Code W2";
          RecWeighing_L."Weight 1 Manual Update" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Weight 1 Manual Update";
          RecWeighing_L."Weight 1 Manual Entry" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Weight 1 Manual Entry";
          RecWeighing_L."Weight 2 Manual Update" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Weight 2 Manual Update";
          RecWeighing_L."Weight 2 Manual Entry" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Weight 2 Manual Entry";
          RecWeighing_L."Manual Weighing No." := "Manual Weighing No.";
          RecWeighing_L."Notebook Weighing No." := "Notebook Weighing No.";
          RecWeighing_L.MODIFY;
          RecTransferDelivery_L."Manual Weighing No." := RecWeighing_L."Manual Weighing No.";
          RecTransferDelivery_L."Notebook Weighing No." := RecWeighing_L."Notebook Weighing No.";
        END;
        RecTransferDelivery_L.MODIFY;

        //Lancement de la validation
        PostedTransferDeliveryNo_L := ValidateDelivery(RecTransferDelivery_L);

        //Archivage de la livraison transfert collecte IC
        ArchiveDeliveryInbox(RecInboxPostedDelivery_P,PostedTransferDeliveryNo_L);

        //Insertion dans la table des correspondances document IC
        InsertICDocumentMatching(PostedTransferDeliveryNo_L,AllMandatoryValueOk_L);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateTransferReceiptDoc@8053016(RecInboxPostReceipt_P@8053000 : Record 8053290);
    VAR
      RecTransferReceipt_L@8053001 : Record 8052993;
      RecCMCSetup_L@8053003 : Record 8052982;
      RecCellLocation_L@8053004 : Record 8053031;
      RecInboxPostedRcptAnal_L@8053002 : Record 8053298;

      RecInboxPostWeighing_L@8053007 : Record 8053302;
      RecWeighing_L@8053008 : Record 8053094;

      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecInboxPostedDelivery_P : livraison transfert collecte de la boOte de r'ception IC
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      WITH RecInboxPostReceipt_P DO BEGIN
        RecTransferReceipt_L.INIT;
        RecTransferReceipt_L."Receipt Type" := RecTransferReceipt_L."Receipt Type"::Transfert;
        RecTransferReceipt_L."IC Direction" := RecTransferReceipt_L."IC Direction"::Incoming;

        // --> 20150702-GEN15/001-TER01.02.01-1854-FBE01

        RecTransferReceipt_L.SetHideValidationDialog(FALSE);

        RecCMCSetup_L.GET;
        RecCMCSetup_L.TESTFIELD("Indep. Transfer Transit Code");
        RecTransferReceipt_L.VALIDATE("Indep. Transfer Transit Code",RecCMCSetup_L."Indep. Transfer Transit Code");
        RecTransferReceipt_L.VALIDATE("Transfer-from Code","Transfer-from Code");

        //RecTransferReceipt_L.VALIDATE("Species Code","Species Code");
        RecTransferReceipt_L.VALIDATE("Species Code" , GetSpeciesCorrespondence("IC Partner Code","Species Code",0));
        RecTransferReceipt_L."Variety Code" := GetVarietyCorrespondence("IC Partner Code","Variety Code",0);

        RecTransferReceipt_L."IC Source Document No." := "No.";
        RecTransferReceipt_L."Manual Transfer No." := "Manual Transfer No.";

        RecTransferReceipt_L.VALIDATE("Trailers Registration","Trailers Registration");
        RecTransferReceipt_L."Transport Method" := "Transport Method";
        RecTransferReceipt_L."Clean Containing" := "Clean Containing";

        // --> 20150206-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-2661-DRN01
        RecTransferReceipt_L."Shipping Agent Code" := GetShippingAgentCorrespondence("IC Partner Code","Shipping Agent Code");
        // <-- 20150206-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-2661-DRN01


        // --> 20150611-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-SDE01
        IF RecShippingAgent_L.GET(RecTransferReceipt_L."Shipping Agent Code") THEN
          RecTransferReceipt_L."Shipping Vendor Code":=RecShippingAgent_L."Vendor No.";

        RecTransferReceipt_L."Shipping Order No." := GetICDocumentMatching("Shipping Order No.",1,"IC Partner Code",
                               RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type"::"Shipping Document");

          IF RecShippingOrder_L.GET(RecTransferReceipt_L."Shipping Order No.") AND (RecShippingOrder_L."Charter No"<>'')
          THEN RecTransferReceipt_L."Charter No.":=RecShippingOrder_L."Charter No";

        RecTransferReceipt_L."Trucks Registration" :=  "Trucks Registration";

          GetCMCSetup;

        END;
        RecTransferReceipt_L."Manual Voucher No." := "Manual Voucher No.";

          GetCMCSetup;
          IF RecCMCSetup_G."Mandatory Manual Voucher No." THEN BEGIN
            RecLocation_L.GET("Location Code");
            IF NOT RecLocation_L."Computer Link Site/Siege" THEN
              RecTransferReceipt_L."Manual Voucher No." := '9999999999';
          END;
        END;
        // <-- 20150616-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-463-FBE01

        RecTransferReceipt_L."Trailers Registration" := "Trailers Registration";
        IF RecTransferReceipt_L."Trailers Registration" =  '' THEN
          RecTransferReceipt_L."Trailers Registration" := '9999999999' ;
        // <-- 20150702-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-1854-FBE03


        //Donn'es autres . r'cup'rer ?
        //RecTransferReceipt_L.Area := Area;

        RecTransferReceipt_L.Disinsectization := RecTransferReceipt_L.Disinsectization::No;
        // --> 20150624-CER170-TER01.01.00-448-FBE01

        // --> 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
        RecTransferReceipt_L."Actual Location Code" := "Actual Location Code";
        // <-- 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE

        //D'sinsectisation
        // --> 20150702-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-1854-FBE04
        //RecTransferReceipt_L."Disinsectization Input" := "Disinsectization Input";
        RecTransferReceipt_L."Disinsectization Input" := RecTransferReceipt_L."Disinsectization Input"::" ";
        //RecTransferReceipt_L."Disinsectization Code Input" := "Disinsectization Code Input";
        //RecTransferReceipt_L."Disinsectization Dosage Input" := "Disinsectization Dosage Input";
        //RecTransferReceipt_L."Period Input" := "Period Input";
        //RecTransferReceipt_L."Pump Code" := "Pump Code";

        //Cr'ation des analyses
        AllMandatoryValueOk_L := FALSE;
        RecTransferRcptAnalysis_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Receipt Type","Receipt No.","Analysis Code");
        RecTransferRcptAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Receipt Type",RecTransferReceipt_L."Receipt Type");
        RecTransferRcptAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Receipt No.",RecTransferReceipt_L."No.");

          AnalysisCode_L := GetAnalysisCorrespondence("IC Partner Code",RecTransferRcptAnalysis_L."Analysis Code",1);

        UNTIL RecTransferRcptAnalysis_L.NEXT = 0;

        // Cr'ation de la pes'e

        RecWeighing_L."Toggle Bridge Code W1" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Toggle Bridge Code W1";
        RecWeighing_L."Toggle Bridge Code W2" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Toggle Bridge Code W2";
        RecWeighing_L."Weight 1 Manual Update" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Weight 1 Manual Update";
        RecWeighing_L."Weight 1 Manual Entry" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Weight 1 Manual Entry";

        RecWeighing_L."Weight 2 Manual Entry" := RecInboxPostWeighing_L."Weight 2 Manual Entry";
        RecWeighing_L."Manual Weighing No." := "Manual Weighing No.";
        RecWeighing_L."Notebook Weighing No." := "Notebook Weighing No.";
        RecWeighing_L.MODIFY;
        RecTransferReceipt_L."Manual Weighing No." := RecWeighing_L."Manual Weighing No.";
        RecTransferReceipt_L."Notebook Weighing No." := RecWeighing_L."Notebook Weighing No.";
