    PROCEDURE CreateShippingOrder@1000000013(RecInboxShipOrder_P@1000000005 : Record 8053471);
    VAR
      RecHandledInBoxShipOrder_L@1000000002 : Record 8053472;
      RecShippingOrder_L@1000000001 : Record 8053265;
      CduCMCStatusMgt_L@1000000003 : Codeunit 8052985;
      RecMcMasterDataRel_L@1000000007 : Record 8053076;
      CduMCDataMgt_L@1000000006 : Codeunit 8053306;
      RecIcPartner_L@1000000009 : Record 413;
      VendorCode_L@1000000004 : Code[20];
      ThirdPartyCode_L@1000000008 : Code[20];
      ShippingCode_L@1000000000 : Code[20];
      RecShippingAgent_L@1000000010 : Record 291;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cr'e et lance un ordre de chargement . partir de la boOte de r'ception IC
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecInboxShipOrder_P :=
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      WITH RecInboxShipOrder_P DO BEGIN
        RecShippingOrder_L.INIT;
        RecShippingOrder_L.INSERT(TRUE);

        RecShippingOrder_L."Shipping Type" := "Shipping Type";
        RecShippingOrder_L.Description := Description;
        //RecShippingOrder_L."Responsable Code" := "Responsable Code";
        //RecShippingOrder_L."Logistic Responsable Code" := "Logistic Responsable Code";
        RecShippingOrder_L.VALIDATE("Campain No.","Campain No.");
        RecShippingOrder_L."Starting Period Start" := "Starting Period Start";
        RecShippingOrder_L."Starting Period End"   := "Starting Period End";
        RecShippingOrder_L."Arrived Period Start"  := "Arrived Period Start";
        RecShippingOrder_L."Arrived Period End"    := "Arrived Period End";
        RecShippingOrder_L."Origin Document Type"  := "Origin Document Type";
        RecShippingOrder_L."Origin Document No."   := "Origin Document No.";
        RecShippingOrder_L."Origin Document Line No." := "Origin Document Line No.";
        // --> 20150610-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-SDE01
        RecShippingOrder_L."IC Source Document No.":=RecInboxShipOrder_P."No.";
        // <-- 20150610-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-SDE01
        RecShippingOrder_L.VALIDATE("Item No." ,GetItemCorrespondence("IC Partner Code", "Item No."));
        RecShippingOrder_L."Variant Code"  := "Variant Code";
        RecShippingOrder_L.Quantity := Quantity;
        RecShippingOrder_L."Shipping Type Capacity"  := "Shipping Type Capacity";
        RecShippingOrder_L."Number Of Shipping Type" := "Number Of Shipping Type";
        // Le "From type" est obligatoirement Location, sinon il n'y aurait pas de flux IC
        RecShippingOrder_L.VALIDATE("From Type","From Type");
        // <-- 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
        RecShippingOrder_L."IC Direction" := RecShippingOrder_L."IC Direction"::Incoming;
        RecShippingOrder_L."On Behalf Of" := TRUE;
        // --> 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
        // --> 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        RecShippingOrder_L."IC Partner Code" := "IC Partner Code";
        // <-- 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        RecShippingOrder_L.VALIDATE("From Code","From Code");
        RecShippingOrder_L."From Adress Code" := "From Adress Code";
        // Le "Destination type" est obligatoirement Customer ou Location, sinon il n'y aurait pas de flux IC
        RecShippingOrder_L.VALIDATE("Destination Type","Destination Type");
        IF "Destination Type" =  "Destination Type"::Customer THEN BEGIN
          // recupere le tiers MS correspondant au code client dans la diffusion des tiers MS pour la soci't' d'origine
          ThirdPartyCode_L := RecMcMasterDataRel_L.RetrieveThirdPartyFromCustVend(
                                   "Destination Code",RecIcPartner_L.GetMcCompanyCode("IC Partner Code"),0);
          // recupere le code client correspondant au tiers MS dans la diffusion des tiers MS pour la soci't' destinataire
          VendorCode_L := RecMcMasterDataRel_L.RetrieveCustVendFromThirdParty(
                                           ThirdPartyCode_L, CduMCDataMgt_L.GetMCCompanyCode(''),0);
          IF VendorCode_L = '' THEN
            ERROR(Text8053005,"Destination Code",RecShippingOrder_L.TABLECAPTION) ;
          RecShippingOrder_L.VALIDATE("Destination Code",VendorCode_L);
        END ELSE
          RecShippingOrder_L.VALIDATE("Destination Code","Destination Code");
        RecShippingOrder_L."Destination Adress Code" := "Destination Adress Code";
        RecShippingOrder_L."Shipping Organisation" := "Shipping Organisation";
        // recupere le tiers MS correspondant au code fournisseur dans la diffusion des tiers MS pour la soci't' d'origine
        ThirdPartyCode_L := RecMcMasterDataRel_L.RetrieveThirdPartyFromCustVend(
                                 "Shipping Vendor Code",RecIcPartner_L.GetMcCompanyCode("IC Partner Code"),1);
        // recupere le code fournisseur correspondant au tiers MS dans la diffusion des tiers MS pour la soci't' destinataire
        ShippingCode_L := RecMcMasterDataRel_L.RetrieveCustVendFromThirdParty(
                                         ThirdPartyCode_L, CduMCDataMgt_L.GetMCCompanyCode(''),1);
        //RecShippingOrder_L.VALIDATE("Shipping Vendor Code","Shipping Vendor Code");
        RecShippingOrder_L.VALIDATE("Shipping Vendor Code",ShippingCode_L );
        RecShippingOrder_L."Shipping Mode" :=  "Shipping Mode";
        // --> 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        //RecShippingOrder_L."IC Partner Code" := "IC Partner Code";
        // <-- 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
        //RecShippingOrder_L."Ship. Purchase Order No."
        //RecShippingOrder_L."Purchase Invoice Type"
        //RecShippingOrder_L."Ship. Purchase Order Line No."

        RecShippingOrder_L."Balance Date"             := "Balance Date";
        RecShippingOrder_L."Grading Start"            := "Grading Start";
        RecShippingOrder_L."Update Date"              := "Update Date";
        RecShippingOrder_L."Deletion Date"            := "Deletion Date";
        RecShippingOrder_L."Delivery Method"          := "Delivery Method";
        RecShippingOrder_L."Date of Arrival at Port"  := "Date of Arrival at Port";
        RecShippingOrder_L."Inspector Name"           := "Inspector Name";
        RecShippingOrder_L."Name Barge"               := "Name Barge";
        RecShippingOrder_L.VALIDATE("Transport Method", "Transport Method");
        RecShippingOrder_L.VALIDATE("Transport Type","Transport Type");

        // --> 20150619-Gen15/001-TER01.01.00-463-FBE
        RecShippingOrder_L."Shipping Agent Code" := GetShippingAgentCorrespondence("IC Partner Code","Shipping Agent Code");
        IF RecShippingAgent_L.GET(RecShippingOrder_L."Shipping Agent Code") THEN
          RecShippingOrder_L."Shipping Vendor Code":=RecShippingAgent_L."Vendor No.";
        // <-- 20150619-Gen15/001-TER01.01.00-463-FBE

        // --> 20150831-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2067-FBE01
        RecShippingOrder_L."Invoice Text"    := "Invoice Text";
        RecShippingOrder_L."Quality comment" := "Quality comment";
        // <-- 20150831-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2067-FBE01

        RecShippingOrder_L.MODIFY;

        //Lancement de l'ordre de chargement
        CduCMCStatusMgt_L.ReleaseShippingOrder(RecShippingOrder_L);

        //Archivage de l'ordre de chargement de la boite de reception IC g'r'e
        RecHandledInBoxShipOrder_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInboxShipOrder_P);
        RecHandledInBoxShipOrder_L.INSERT;

        //Insertion dans la table des correspondances document IC
        InsertICDocumentMatching(RecShippingOrder_L."No.",TRUE);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CancelCollectReceiptDoc@8053005(RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P@8053002 : Record 8053290);
    VAR
      RecICDocumentMatching_L@8053014 : Record 8053305;
      RecPostedReceipt_L@8053001 : Record 8052995;
      RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_L@8053010 : Record 8053290;
      RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L@8053009 : Record 8053294;
      RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L@8053008 : Record 8053298;
      RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L@8053007 : Record 8053302;
      RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L@8053006 : Record 8053291;
      RecHandedlInboxRcptLine_L@8053005 : Record 8053295;
      RecHDInBoxCollectAnalysis_L@8053004 : Record 8053299;
      RecHDInBoxCollectWeighing_L@8053003 : Record 8053303;
      CduPostReceipt_L@8053000 : Codeunit 8052984;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Annule la r'ception c'r'ales . partir de la reception c'r'ales enregistr'e de la boOte de reception IC
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P : En-t^te reception c'r'ale boOte de r'ception IC
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      GetCompanyInfo;
      CompanyInfo.TESTFIELD("IC Partner Code");

      WITH RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P DO BEGIN
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Source IC Partner","Source Type","Source Document Type","Source Document No.");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source IC Partner",RecICInboxTrans_G."IC Partner Code");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Type",RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Type"::"Purchase Collect Document");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Document Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document Type");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Document No.",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document No.");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.FINDFIRST ; // Pas de gestion d'erreur, la correspondance doit exister
        // --> 20150902-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2184-FBE01
        //RecPostedReceipt_L.GET(RecPostedReceipt_L."Receipt Type"::Receipt,RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document No.");
        RecPostedReceipt_L.GET("Receipt Type",RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document No.");
        // <-- 20150902-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2184-FBE01

        // Passage Inbox entete reception vers Inbox g'r'e entete reception
        RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInboxCollectRcptHeader_P);
        RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L."No." := RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document No." ;
        RecHandledInboxRcptHeader_L.INSERT;

        // Passage Inbox lignes de reception vers Inbox g'r'e lignes reception
        RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.SETCURRENTKEY("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source","Line No.");
        RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.","IC Transaction No.");
        RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.SETRANGE("Transaction Source","Transaction Source");
        RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code","IC Partner Code");
        IF RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            RecHandedlInboxRcptLine_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L);
            RecHandedlInboxRcptLine_L."No." := RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document No." ;
            RecHandedlInboxRcptLine_L.INSERT;
          UNTIL RecInboxCollectRcptLine_L.NEXT = 0;

        // Passage Inbox lignes d'analyse vers Inbox g'r'e lignes d'analyse
        RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.SETCURRENTKEY("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source","Analysis Code");
        RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.","IC Transaction No.");
        RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Transaction Source","Transaction Source");
        RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code","IC Partner Code");
        IF RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
              RecHDInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L);
              RecHDInBoxCollectAnalysis_L."Receipt No." := RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document No." ;
              RecHDInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.INSERT;
          UNTIL RecInBoxCollectAnalysis_L.NEXT = 0;

        // Passage Inbox pes'e vers Inbox g'r'e pes'e
        RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L.SETCURRENTKEY("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source");
        RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.","IC Transaction No.");
        RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L.SETRANGE("Transaction Source","Transaction Source");
        RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code","IC Partner Code");
        IF RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            RecHDInBoxCollectWeighing_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L);
            RecHDInBoxCollectWeighing_L."Source Document No." := RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document No." ;
            RecHDInBoxCollectWeighing_L.INSERT;
          UNTIL RecInBoxCollectWeighing_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;
      CduPostReceipt_L.SetHideValidationDialog(TRUE);
      CduPostReceipt_L.SetForceCancel(TRUE);
      CduPostReceipt_L.CancelPostedReceipt(RecPostedReceipt_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CancelTransferDelivery@8053019(RecInboxPostedDelivery_P@8053000 : Record 8053340);
    VAR
      RecPostedTransferDelivery_L@8053001 : Record 8053014;
      RecICDocumentMatching_L@8053014 : Record 8053305;
      cduPostDelivery_L@8053015 : Codeunit 8052988;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Annule la livraison transfert collecte . partir de la boOte de r'ception IC
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecInboxPostedDelivery_P : livraison transfert collecte de la boOte de r'ception IC
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      WITH RecInboxPostedDelivery_P DO BEGIN
        //Recherche de la livraison transfert . annuler dans la table de correspondance document IC
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Source IC Partner","Source Type","Source Document Type","Source Document No.");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source IC Partner",RecICInboxTrans_G."IC Partner Code");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Document Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document Type");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Document No.",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document No.");
        GetCompanyInfo;
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination IC Partner",CompanyInfo."IC Partner Code");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.FINDFIRST ; // Pas de gestion d'erreur, la correspondance doit exister
        RecPostedTransferDelivery_L.GET("Delivery Type",RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document No.");

        //Lancement de l'annulation
        //Neutraliser les dialogues avec l'utilisateur
        cduPostDelivery_L.SetHideValidationDialog(TRUE);
        //Neutraliser le bloquage de l'annulation manuelle d'un transfert IC et la proposition de cr'ation d'un nouveau transfert
        cduPostDelivery_L.CallFromPostICTransfer(TRUE);
        cduPostDelivery_L.CancelPostedIndepTransfer(RecPostedTransferDelivery_L);

        //Archivage de la livraison transfert collecte IC
        ArchiveDeliveryInbox(RecInboxPostedDelivery_P,RecPostedTransferDelivery_L."No.");
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ReconcileTransferDelivery@8053015(RecInboxPostedDelivery_P@8053000 : Record 8053340);
    VAR
      RecPostedTransferDelivery_L@8053001 : Record 8053014;
      RecICDocumentMatching_L@8053014 : Record 8053305;
      RecICDocumentMatching2_L@8053002 : Record 8053305;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Rapproche la livraison transfert collecte . partir de la boOte de r'ception IC
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecInboxPostedDelivery_P : livraison transfert collecte de la boOte de r'ception IC
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      WITH RecInboxPostedDelivery_P DO BEGIN
        //Recherche de la livraison transfert . rapprocher dans la table de correspondance document IC
        GetCompanyInfo;
        IF "On Behalf Of Adjustment" AND (CompanyInfo."IC Partner Code" = "IC Partner-From Code") THEN BEGIN
          RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Destination IC Partner","Destination Type","Destination Document Type",
            "Destination Document No.");
          RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination IC Partner","IC Partner Code");
          RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type");
          RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination Document Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document Type");
          RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination Document No.",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document No.");
          RecICDocumentMatching_L.FINDFIRST; // Pas de gestion d'erreur, la correspondance doit exister

          RecICDocumentMatching2_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Destination IC Partner","Destination Type","Destination Document Type",
            "Source Document No.");
          RecICDocumentMatching2_L.SETRANGE("Destination IC Partner","IC Partner-From Code");
          RecICDocumentMatching2_L.SETRANGE("Destination Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type");
          RecICDocumentMatching2_L.SETRANGE("Destination Document Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document Type");
          RecICDocumentMatching2_L.SETRANGE("Source Document No.",RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Document No.");
          RecICDocumentMatching2_L.FINDFIRST; // Pas de gestion d'erreur, la correspondance doit exister
          RecPostedTransferDelivery_L.GET("Delivery Type",RecICDocumentMatching2_L."Destination Document No.");
        END ELSE BEGIN
          RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Destination IC Partner","Destination Type","Destination Document Type",
            "Destination Document No.");
          RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination IC Partner",RecICInboxTrans_G."IC Partner Code");
          RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type");
          RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination Document Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document Type");
          RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination Document No.",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document No.");
          RecICDocumentMatching_L.FINDFIRST ; // Pas de gestion d'erreur, la correspondance doit exister
          RecPostedTransferDelivery_L.GET("Delivery Type",RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Document No.");
        END;

        //Validation du rapprochement
        RecPostedTransferDelivery_L."Reconciliation Validated" := TRUE;
        RecPostedTransferDelivery_L.MODIFY;

        //Archivage de la livraison transfert collecte IC
        ArchiveDeliveryInbox(RecInboxPostedDelivery_P,RecPostedTransferDelivery_L."No.");
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ReconcileTransferReceipt@8053028(RecInboxPostReceipt_P@8053000 : Record 8053290);
    VAR
      RecPostedTransferReceipt_L@8053001 : Record 8052995;
      RecICDocumentMatching_L@8053014 : Record 8053305;

      // Sorties:

          "Destination Document No.");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination IC Partner",RecICInboxTrans_G."IC Partner Code");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination Document Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document Type");

      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ValidateReceipt@1000000004(RecCollectReceipt_P@8053002 : Record 8052993) PostedReceiptNo_P : Code[20];

      PostedReceiptNo_P := CduPostReceipt_L.GetPostedReceipt;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ValidateItemJnlLine@1000000010(RecItemJnlLine_P@8053002 : Record 83) : Integer;

      ItemLedgerEntryNo_L@1000000000 : Integer;

      CduPostItemJnlLine_L@1000000002 : Codeunit 22;

    BEGIN

    PROCEDURE InsertICDocumentMatching@8053004(DestinationDocumentNo_P@1000000001 : Code[20];OwnerOk_P@1000000002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      RecICDocumentMatching_L@1000000000 : Record 8053305;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Insere un enregistrement dans la table IC Document Matching (T8053305)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - DestinationDocumentNo_P : N› document destination
      //   - OwnerOk_P : Les conditions pour changement du proprietaire sont remplies
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      RecICDocumentMatching_L.LOCKTABLE;
      RecICDocumentMatching_L.INIT;
      RecICDocumentMatching_L."Entry No." := 0;
      RecICDocumentMatching_L."GUID No." := CREATEGUID;
      RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source IC Partner" := RecICInboxTrans_G."IC Partner Code";
      RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Document Type" := RecICInboxTrans_G."Document Type";
      RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Document No." := RecICInboxTrans_G."Document No.";
      RecICDocumentMatching_L.Owner := RecICDocumentMatching_L.Owner::Source;
      CASE TRUE OF
        // L'origine du flux IC est un document vente classique (Commande achat, Retour vente)
        RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type" = RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type"::"Purchase Document" :
          BEGIN
            // Flux IC standard
            // Commande vente -> Commande achat
            // Retour vente -> Retour achat
            // Facture vente -> Facture achat
            // Avoir vente -> Avoir achat
            RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Type" := RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Type"::"Sales Document";
            RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Type" := RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type";
            RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document Type" := RecICInboxTrans_G."Document Type";
          END;
        // L'origine du flux IC est un document achat classique  (Commande vente, Retour achat)
        RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type" = RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type"::"Sales Document" :
          BEGIN
            // Flux IC standard
            // Commande achat -> Commande vente
            // Retour achat -> Retour vente
            // Facture achat -> Facture vente
            // Avoir achat -> Avoir vente
            RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Type" := RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Type"::"Purchase Document";
            RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Type" := RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type";
            RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document Type" := RecICInboxTrans_G."Document Type";
          END;
        RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type" IN [
          RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type"::"Purchase Collect Document",
          RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type"::"Transfer Collect Document",
          RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type"::"Item Journal Line"] :
          BEGIN
            // Flux IC r'ception et transfert collecte c'r'ales
            RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Type" := RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type";
            RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Type" := RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type";
            RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document Type" := RecICInboxTrans_G."Document Type";
            IF OwnerOk_P THEN
              RecICDocumentMatching_L.Owner := RecICDocumentMatching_L.Owner::Destination;
          END;
        RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type" IN [RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type"::"Shipping Document"] :
          BEGIN
            // Flux IC ordre de chargement
            RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Type" := RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type";
            RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Type" := RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type";
            RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document Type" := RecICInboxTrans_G."Document Type";
            RecICDocumentMatching_L.Owner := RecICDocumentMatching_L.Owner::Destination;
          END;
        RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type" IN [RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type"::"Sales Collect Document" ] :
          BEGIN
            // Flux IC bon de sortie c'r'ales
            RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Type" := RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type";
            RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Type" := RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type";
            RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document Type" := RecICInboxTrans_G."Document Type";
            IF OwnerOk_P THEN
              RecICDocumentMatching_L.Owner := RecICDocumentMatching_L.Owner::Destination;
          END;
      END;
      GetCompanyInfo;
      RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination IC Partner" := CompanyInfo."IC Partner Code";
      RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document No." := DestinationDocumentNo_P;
      RecICDocumentMatching_L.INSERT;
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsertICDocMatchingOutBase@1000000023(RecOutboxTrans_P@1000000003 : Record 414;OwnerOk_P@1000000002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      RecICDocumentMatching_L@1000000000 : Record 8053305;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Insere un enregistrement dans la table IC Document Matching (T8053305) pour un flux sortant hors base
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecOutboxTrans_P : Record transaction sortante
      //   - OwnerOk_P : Les conditions pour changement du proprietaire sont remplies
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      RecICDocumentMatching_L.LOCKTABLE;
      RecICDocumentMatching_L.INIT;
      RecICDocumentMatching_L."Entry No." := 0;
      RecICDocumentMatching_L."GUID No." := CREATEGUID;
      GetCompanyInfo;
      RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Type" := RecOutboxTrans_P."Source Type";
      RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source IC Partner" := CompanyInfo."IC Partner Code";
      RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Document Type" := RecOutboxTrans_P."Document Type";
      RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Document No." := RecOutboxTrans_P."Document No.";
      RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination IC Partner" := RecOutboxTrans_P."IC Partner Code";
      RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Type" := RecOutboxTrans_P."Source Type";
      RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document Type" := RecOutboxTrans_P."Document Type";
      IF OwnerOk_P THEN
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.Owner := RecICDocumentMatching_L.Owner::Destination
      ELSE
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.Owner := RecICDocumentMatching_L.Owner::Source;
      RecICDocumentMatching_L."Outside Database" := TRUE;
      RecICDocumentMatching_L.INSERT;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetICDocumentMatching@1000000021(DocumentNo_P@1000000001 : Code[20];Direction_P@1000000002 : 'Source,Destination';ICPartnerCode_P@1000000004 : Code[20];TypeMatching_P@1000000005 : ' ,Sales Document,Purchase Document,Purchase Collect Document,Sales Collect Document,Transfer Collect Document,Item Journal Line,Shipping Document') : Code[20];
    VAR
      RecICDocumentMatching_L@1000000000 : Record 8053305;
      CorrespondanceDocNo_L@1000000003 : Code[20];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Renvoie le document correspondant . celui pass' en parametre
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //  - DocumentNo_P    : N› de document dont on veut trouver la correspondance
      //  - Direction_P     : Source ou destination
      //  - ICPartnerCode_P : Code du partenaire
      //  - TypeMatching_P  : = source type ou destination type
      //
      // Sorties:
      //   - CorrespondanceDocNo_L : Le N› de document Trouv'
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      WITH RecICDocumentMatching_L DO BEGIN
        RESET;
        CASE Direction_P OF
          Direction_P::Source:
          BEGIN
            SETCURRENTKEY("Source IC Partner","Source Type","Source Document Type","Source Document No.");
            SETRANGE("Source IC Partner",ICPartnerCode_P);
            SETRANGE("Source Type", TypeMatching_P);
            SETRANGE("Source Document No.",DocumentNo_P);
            IF NOT FINDFIRST THEN
              INIT;
            CorrespondanceDocNo_L := "Destination Document No.";
          END;
          Direction_P::Destination :
          BEGIN
            SETCURRENTKEY("Destination IC Partner","Destination Type",
                                                            "Destination Document Type","Destination Document No.");
            SETRANGE("Destination IC Partner",ICPartnerCode_P);
            SETRANGE("Destination Type",TypeMatching_P);
            SETRANGE("Destination Document No.",DocumentNo_P);
            IF NOT FINDFIRST THEN
              INIT;
            CorrespondanceDocNo_L := "Source Document No.";
          END;
        END;
      END;
      EXIT(CorrespondanceDocNo_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetItemCorrespondence@8053034(ICPartnerCode_P@8053002 : Code[20];ItemNo_P@8053003 : Code[20]) : Code[20];
    VAR
      RecDataCorrespondence_L@8053001 : Record 8053304;
      CduEngineMgt_L@8053000 : Codeunit 8061230;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Retourne la correspondance article du partenaire IC
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - ICPartnerCode_P : Partenaire IC
      //   - ItemNo_P : N› article
      // Sorties:
      //   - N› article correspondant. Si pas de correspondance, renvoie la valeur pass'e en paramStre (ItemNo_P)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF RecDataCorrespondence_L.GET(ICPartnerCode_P,0,RecDataCorrespondence_L."Correspondance Type"::Item) THEN
        EXIT(CduEngineMgt_L.GetReferentialCorrespondance(RecDataCorrespondence_L."Correspondence Template",TRUE,ItemNo_P,FALSE));
      EXIT(ItemNo_P);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetAnalysisCorrespondence@8053045(ICPartnerCode_P@8053002 : Code[20];AnalysisCode_P@8053003 : Code[10];Direction_P@1000000000 : 'Input,Ouput') : Code[20];
    VAR
      RecDataCorrespondence_L@8053001 : Record 8053304;
      CduEngineMgt_L@8053000 : Codeunit 8061230;
      RecAnalysisCaract_L@8053004 : Record 8053092;
      Import_L@1000000001 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Retourne la correspondance analyse du partenaire IC
      //------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - ICPartnerCode_P : Partenaire IC
      //   - AnalysisCode_P : Code analyse
      //   - Direction_P    : Le sens de r'cup'ration de la correspondance
      //                      Si Input recherche "Valeur Interface"
      //                      Si Output, recherche "Valeur NAV"
      // Sorties:
      //   - Code analyses correspondant. Si pas de correspondance, renvoie la valeur pass'e en paramStre (AnalysisCode_P)
      //------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF Direction_P = Direction_P::Input THEN
        Import_L := TRUE
      ELSE
        Import_L := FALSE;
      IF RecDataCorrespondence_L.GET(ICPartnerCode_P,0,RecDataCorrespondence_L."Correspondance Type"::Global) THEN
        EXIT(CduEngineMgt_L.GetCorresEmptyInclude(RecDataCorrespondence_L."Correspondence Template",
              RecAnalysisCaract_L.TABLENAME,RecAnalysisCaract_L.FIELDNAME(RecAnalysisCaract_L.Code),AnalysisCode_P,Import_L,FALSE));
      EXIT(AnalysisCode_P);
    END;

    PROCEDURE ArchiveDeliveryInbox@8053039(RecInboxPostedDelivery_P@8053000 : Record 8053340;PostedDeliveryNo_P@8053001 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      RecInboxPostedDelAnal_L@8053007 : Record 8053344;
      RecHandInboxPostDelAna_L@8053006 : Record 8053345;
      RecInboxPostWeighing_L@8053004 : Record 8053302;
      RecHandledInboxPostWeighing_L@8053003 : Record 8053303;
      RecHandledInboxPostDel_L@8053002 : Record 8053341;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Archive la livraison collecte de la boOte de r'ception IC vers la boOte de r'ception IC g'r'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecInboxPostedDelivery_P : livraison collecte de la boOte de r'ception IC
      //   - PostedDeliveryNo_P := N› livraison valid'e
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      WITH RecInboxPostedDelivery_P DO BEGIN
        //Archivage de la livraison transfert collecte IC
        RecHandledInboxPostDel_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInboxPostedDelivery_P);
        RecHandledInboxPostDel_L."No." := PostedDeliveryNo_P;
        RecHandledInboxPostDel_L.INSERT;

        //Archivage de la pes'e IC
        //Cas du transfert de propri't' -> pas d'enregistrement de pes'e
        IF RecInboxPostWeighing_L.GET("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source") THEN BEGIN
          RecHandledInboxPostWeighing_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInboxPostWeighing_L);
          RecHandledInboxPostWeighing_L."Source Document No." := PostedDeliveryNo_P;
          RecHandledInboxPostWeighing_L.INSERT;
        END;

        //Archivage des analyses IC
        RecInboxPostedDelAnal_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.","IC Transaction No.");
        RecInboxPostedDelAnal_L.SETRANGE("Transaction Source","Transaction Source");
        RecInboxPostedDelAnal_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code","IC Partner Code");
        IF RecInboxPostedDelAnal_L.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            RecHandInboxPostDelAna_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInboxPostedDelAnal_L);
            RecHandInboxPostDelAna_L."Delivery No." := PostedDeliveryNo_P;
            RecHandInboxPostDelAna_L.INSERT;
          UNTIL RecInboxPostedDelAnal_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ArchiveReceiptInbox@8053057(RecInboxPostReceipt_P@8053005 : Record 8053290;PostedReceiptNo_P@8053000 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      RecInboxPostedRcptAnal_L@8053012 : Record 8053298;
      RecHandInboxPostRcptAna_L@8053011 : Record 8053299;
      RecInboxPostWeighing_L@8053010 : Record 8053302;
      RecHandledInboxPostWeighing_L@8053009 : Record 8053303;
      RecHandledInboxPostRcpt_L@8053008 : Record 8053291;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Archive la r'ception collecte de la boOte de r'ception IC vers la boOte de r'ception IC g'r'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecInboxPostReceipt_P : r'ception collecte de la boOte de r'ception IC
      //   - PostedReceiptNo_P := N› r'ception valid'e
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      WITH RecInboxPostReceipt_P DO BEGIN
        //Archivage de la r'ception transfert collecte IC
        RecHandledInboxPostRcpt_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInboxPostReceipt_P);
        RecHandledInboxPostRcpt_L."No." := PostedReceiptNo_P;
        RecHandledInboxPostRcpt_L.INSERT;

        //Archivage de la pes'e IC
        RecInboxPostWeighing_L.GET("IC Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source");
        RecHandledInboxPostWeighing_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInboxPostWeighing_L);
        RecHandledInboxPostWeighing_L."Source Document No." := PostedReceiptNo_P;
        RecHandledInboxPostWeighing_L.INSERT;

        //Archivage des analyses IC
        RecInboxPostedRcptAnal_L.SETRANGE("IC Transaction No.","IC Transaction No.");
        RecInboxPostedRcptAnal_L.SETRANGE("Transaction Source","Transaction Source");
        RecInboxPostedRcptAnal_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code","IC Partner Code");
        IF RecInboxPostedRcptAnal_L.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            RecHandInboxPostRcptAna_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInboxPostedRcptAnal_L);
            RecHandInboxPostRcptAna_L."Receipt No." := PostedReceiptNo_P;
            RecHandInboxPostRcptAna_L.INSERT;
          UNTIL RecInboxPostedRcptAnal_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetSetOffLocationFromICPartner@8053014(ICPartnerCode_P@8053000 : Code[20]) : Code[10];

      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
