    PROCEDURE TestPendingICInboxTransfer@8053017(SourceType_P@8053000 : ' ,,,,,Transfer Collect Document';DocumentType_P@8053001 : ' ,,,,,,,,,,Collect Transfer Delivery,Collect Transfer Receipt';DocumentNo_P@8053002 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      RecICDocumentMatching_L@8053003 : Record 8053305;
      RecInboxTransaction_L@8053004 : Record 418;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Test si un flux d'annulation ou de rapprochement d'un transfert IC peut ^tre envoy' au partenaire
      // Cas o- l'annulation est envoy'e alors qu'un message de rapprochement est en attente dans la boOte de
      // r'ception IC (Idem dans l'autre sens)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - SourceType_P : Transfer Collect Document
      //   - DocumentType_P : Collect Transfer Delivery, Collect Transfer Receipt
      //   - DocumentNo_P := N› transfert IC
      // Sorties:
      //   - Erreur si un message est en attente de validation dans la boOte de r'ception IC du partenaire
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      GetCompanyInfo;
      // Recherche sur le partenaire source
      RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Source IC Partner","Source Type","Source Document Type","Source Document No.");
      RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source IC Partner",CompanyInfo."IC Partner Code");
      RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Type",SourceType_P);
      RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Document Type",DocumentType_P);
      RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Document No.",DocumentNo_P);
      IF RecICDocumentMatching_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        RecInboxTransaction_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Source Type","Document Type","Document No.");
        RecInboxTransaction_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code",RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination IC Partner");
        RecInboxTransaction_L.SETRANGE("Source Type",RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Type");
        RecInboxTransaction_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document Type");
        RecInboxTransaction_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document No.");
        IF RecInboxTransaction_L.FINDFIRST THEN
          ERROR(Text8053002,RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document Type",RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document No.",
            RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination IC Partner");
      END ELSE BEGIN
        // Recherche sur le partenaire destination
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.RESET;
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Destination IC Partner","Destination Type","Destination Document Type",
          "Destination Document No.");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination IC Partner",CompanyInfo."IC Partner Code");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination Type",SourceType_P);
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination Document Type",DocumentType_P);
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination Document No.",DocumentNo_P);
        IF RecICDocumentMatching_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          RecInboxTransaction_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Source Type","Document Type","Document No.");
          RecInboxTransaction_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code",RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source IC Partner");
          RecInboxTransaction_L.SETRANGE("Source Type",RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Type");
          RecInboxTransaction_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Document Type");
          RecInboxTransaction_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Document No.");
          IF RecInboxTransaction_L.FINDFIRST THEN
            ERROR(Text8053002,RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Document Type",RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source Document No.",
              RecICDocumentMatching_L."Source IC Partner");
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE AutoSendOutboxJournal@8053027();
    VAR
      RecOutboxJnlTransaction_L@8053000 : Record 414;
      cduICOutboxExport_L@8053001 : Codeunit 431;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Envoie automatiquement le feuille compta. IC au partenaire
      // Appel dans cdu 13, 80, et 90
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   -
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      WITH RecOutboxJnlTransactionTmp_G DO BEGIN
        IF FINDSET THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            RecOutboxJnlTransaction_L.GET("Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Transaction Source","Document Type");
            // Envoi automatique vers la boite de reception IC partenaire
            cduICOutboxExport_L.AutoSendToInternalPartner(RecOutboxJnlTransaction_L);
          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckSharedSiloInventory@8053018(ItemNo_P@8053003 : Code[20];LocationCode_P@8053001 : Code[10];ICPartnerCode_P@8053000 : Code[20];ICFromPartnerCode_P@8053004 : Code[20];StandardWeight_P@8053006 : Decimal);
    VAR
      RecItem_L@8053002 : Record 27;
      ICPartnerCode_L@8053005 : Code[20];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // V'rifie le stock du silo partag' du partenaire IC
      // Appel dans Cdu 8052989 Post Delivery (Yes/No)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - ItemNo_P : N› article
      //   - LocationCode_P : Code du silo partag'
      //   - ICPartnerCode_P : Code du partenaire IC
      //   - ICFromPartnerCode_P : Code du partenaire IC origine
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF ICFromPartnerCode_P <> '' THEN
        //Stock de la soci't' utilisatrice
        ICPartnerCode_L := ICFromPartnerCode_P
      ELSE BEGIN
        //Stock de la soci't' de gestion
        GetCompanyInfo;
        ICPartnerCode_L := CompanyInfo."IC Partner Code";
      END;
      RecItem_L.GET(ItemNo_P);
      RecItem_L.SETRANGE("Location Filter",LocationCode_P);
      RecItem_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Filter",ICFromPartnerCode_P);
      RecItem_L.CALCFIELDS(Inventory);
      IF (RecItem_L.Inventory - StandardWeight_P) < 0 THEN BEGIN
        IF NOT CONFIRM(Text8505003,FALSE,ICPartnerCode_L,LocationCode_P) THEN BEGIN
          ERROR(Text8505004);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CompanyManageSharedSilo@8053020(RecLocation_P@8053000 : Record 14) Res_Bool : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // V'rifie si la soci't' est gestionnaire du silo partag'
      // Appel dans T8052993, T8053013, C21, R8053096
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecLocation_P : record Location
      // Sorties:
      //   - TRUE si la soci't' est gestionnaire, FALSE sinon
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Res_Bool := FALSE;
      WITH RecLocation_P DO BEGIN
        IF NOT "Shared Silo" THEN BEGIN
          Res_Bool := FALSE;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          TESTFIELD("Part IC Shar Silo Manager Code");
          GetCompanyInfo;
          Res_Bool := CompanyInfo."IC Partner Code" = "Part IC Shar Silo Manager Code";
        END;
        EXIT(Res_Bool);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CompanyOutOfBase@1000000020(RecLocation_P@1000000000 : Record 14) : Boolean;
    VAR
      OutOfBase_L@1000000001 : Boolean;
      RecIcPartner_L@1000000002 : Record 413;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // V'rifie si la soci't' est hors base pour le silo de compensation pass' en parametre
      // Appel dans CU21
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecLocation_P : record Location
      // Sorties:
      //   - TRUE si la soci't' hors base, FALSE sinon
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      OutOfBase_L := FALSE;
      RecIcPartner_L.SETRANGE("Set Off Location Code",RecLocation_P.Code);
      IF RecIcPartner_L.FINDFIRST THEN
        OutOfBase_L := RecIcPartner_L."Cereal Flow" = RecIcPartner_L."Cereal Flow"::"No IC Transfer";
      EXIT(OutOfBase_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE TestIcDocumentOwner@1000000011(SourceType_P@1000000001 : 'Journal,Sales Document,Purchase Document,Purchase Collect Document,Sales Collect Document,Transfer Collect Document,Item Journal Line,Shipping Document';DocumentType_P@1000000002 : ' ,Payment,Invoice,Credit Memo,Refund,Order,Return Order,Collect Receipt,Collect Resumption Receipt,Collect Delivery,Collect Transfer Delivery,Collect Transfer Receipt,Inventory line,Shipping Order';DocumentNo_P@1000000004 : Code[20]) : Boolean;
    VAR
      RecICDocumentMatching_L@1000000000 : Record 8053305;
      RecPostCollectReceipt_L@1000000005 : Record 8052995;
      RecIcPartner_L@1000000006 : Record 413;
      Owner_L@1000000003 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Teste si la soci't' est propri'raire du document
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - SourceType_P   : Source du document
      //   - DocumentType_P : Type de document
      //   - DocumentNo_P   : N› du document . rechercher
      // Sorties:
      //   - TRUE, FALSE
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      GetCompanyInfo;

      // Recherche sur le partenaire source
      RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Source IC Partner","Source Type","Source Document Type","Source Document No.");
      RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source IC Partner",CompanyInfo."IC Partner Code");
      RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Type",SourceType_P);
      RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Document Type",DocumentType_P);
      RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Document No.",DocumentNo_P);
      IF RecICDocumentMatching_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        IF RecICDocumentMatching_L.Owner = RecICDocumentMatching_L.Owner::Source THEN
          Owner_L := TRUE
        ELSE
          Owner_L := FALSE;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        // Recherche sur le partenaire destination
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.RESET;
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Destination IC Partner","Destination Type","Destination Document Type",
          "Destination Document No.");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination IC Partner",CompanyInfo."IC Partner Code");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination Type",SourceType_P);
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination Document Type",DocumentType_P);
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination Document No.",DocumentNo_P);
        IF RecICDocumentMatching_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          IF RecICDocumentMatching_L.Owner = RecICDocumentMatching_L.Owner::Destination THEN
            Owner_L := TRUE
          ELSE
            Owner_L := FALSE;
        END;
      END;
      IF NOT Owner_L THEN BEGIN
        CASE DocumentType_P OF
          DocumentType_P::"Collect Receipt":
          BEGIN
            IF RecPostCollectReceipt_L.GET(RecPostCollectReceipt_L."Receipt Type"::Receipt,DocumentNo_P) THEN BEGIN
              IF RecIcPartner_L.GET(RecPostCollectReceipt_L."IC Partner Code") AND
                (RecIcPartner_L."Cereal Flow"  = RecIcPartner_L."Cereal Flow"::"File Location") THEN
                   Owner_L := TRUE;
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;
      EXIT(Owner_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateIcPartnersAllocation@1000000015(RecItemJnlLine_P@1000000000 : Record 83) : Boolean;
    VAR
      RecPartnerAllocate_L@1000000002 : Record 8053328;
      RecMCLocation_L@1000000001 : Record 8053083;
      RecSiloIcPartner_L@1000000003 : Record 8053335;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cr'e les lignes de r'partition Boni/freinte pour les Partenaires du silo
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecItemJnlLine_P  : La ligne de la feuille de boni freinte pour laquelle on veut r'partir
      // Sorties:
      //   - TRUE, FALSE
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      WITH RecItemJnlLine_P DO BEGIN
        TESTFIELD("Allocate on IC Partners");
        TESTFIELD("Item No.");
        IF RecMCLocation_L.GET("Location Code") THEN BEGIN
          RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETRANGE("Journal Template Name","Journal Template Name");
          RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETRANGE("Line No.","Line No.");
          RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETRANGE("Journal Batch Name","Journal Batch Name");
          IF RecPartnerAllocate_L.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
            RecSiloIcPartner_L.SETRANGE("MC Location Code","Location Code");
            IF RecSiloIcPartner_L.FINDSET THEN
            REPEAT
              RecPartnerAllocate_L."Journal Template Name"  := "Journal Template Name";
              RecPartnerAllocate_L."Line No."               := "Line No.";
              RecPartnerAllocate_L."Journal Batch Name"     := "Journal Batch Name";
              RecPartnerAllocate_L."Partner IC Code"        := RecSiloIcPartner_L."IC Partner Code";
              RecPartnerAllocate_L."Cell Code"              := "Cell Code";
              RecPartnerAllocate_L."Location Code"          := "Location Code";
              RecPartnerAllocate_L.INSERT;
            UNTIL RecSiloIcPartner_L.NEXT = 0;
            COMMIT;
          END;
        END;
      END;
      EXIT(TRUE);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreatePartnersCampaignReport@60002(RecItemJnlLine_P@1000000000 : Record 83) : Boolean;
    VAR
      RecPartnerAllocate_L@1000000002 : Record 8053328;
      RecMCLocation_L@1000000001 : Record 8053083;
      RecSiloIcPartner_L@1000000003 : Record 8053335;
      RecItemLedgEntry_L@60000 : Record 32;
      RecCompanyInfo_L@60005 : Record 79;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cr'e les lignes de r'partition report de campagne pour les Partenaires du silo
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecItemJnlLine_P  : La ligne de la feuille de report de campagne pour laquelle on veut r'partir
      // Sorties:
      //   - TRUE, FALSE
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // --> 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
      WITH RecItemJnlLine_P DO BEGIN
        TESTFIELD("Allocate on IC Partners");
        TESTFIELD("Item No.");
        TESTFIELD("Campaign No.");
        RecCompanyInfo_L.GET;
        RecCompanyInfo_L.TESTFIELD("IC Partner Code");
        IF RecMCLocation_L.GET("Location Code") THEN BEGIN
          RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETRANGE("Journal Template Name","Journal Template Name");
          RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETRANGE("Line No.","Line No.");
          RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETRANGE("Journal Batch Name","Journal Batch Name");
          IF RecPartnerAllocate_L.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
            RecSiloIcPartner_L.SETRANGE("MC Location Code","Location Code");
            IF RecSiloIcPartner_L.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
              /// Calcul de la quantit' en stock par partenaire
              RecItemLedgEntry_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Item No.","Location Code","Cell Code","Lot No.",
                                              "Posting Date","Campaign No.","IC Partner Code");
              RecItemLedgEntry_L.SETRANGE("Item No.", "Item No.");
              RecItemLedgEntry_L.SETRANGE("Location Code", "Location Code");
              RecItemLedgEntry_L.SETRANGE("Cell Code","Cell Code");
              IF "Reverse Campaigns" THEN
                RecItemLedgEntry_L.SETRANGE("Campaign No.", "Destination Campaign No.")
              ELSE
                RecItemLedgEntry_L.SETRANGE("Campaign No.", "Campaign No.");
              REPEAT
                RecItemLedgEntry_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code", RecSiloIcPartner_L."IC Partner Code");
                RecItemLedgEntry_L.CALCSUMS(Quantity);
                IF RecItemLedgEntry_L.Quantity <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                  RecPartnerAllocate_L."Journal Template Name"  := "Journal Template Name";
                  RecPartnerAllocate_L."Line No."               := "Line No.";
                  RecPartnerAllocate_L."Journal Batch Name"     := "Journal Batch Name";
                  RecPartnerAllocate_L."Partner IC Code"        := RecSiloIcPartner_L."IC Partner Code";
                  RecPartnerAllocate_L."Cell Code"              := "Cell Code";
                  RecPartnerAllocate_L."Location Code"          := "Location Code";
                  RecPartnerAllocate_L.Quantity                 := RecItemLedgEntry_L.Quantity;
                  RecPartnerAllocate_L.INSERT;
                END;
              UNTIL RecSiloIcPartner_L.NEXT = 0;
              /// Affectation de la quantit' sur le gestionnaire du silo
              RecItemLedgEntry_L.SETFILTER("IC Partner Code" ,'=%1','');
              RecItemLedgEntry_L.CALCSUMS(Quantity);
              IF (RecItemLedgEntry_L.Quantity  <> 0) THEN BEGIN
                RecPartnerAllocate_L."Journal Template Name"  := "Journal Template Name";
                RecPartnerAllocate_L."Line No."               := "Line No.";
                RecPartnerAllocate_L."Journal Batch Name"     := "Journal Batch Name";
                RecPartnerAllocate_L."Partner IC Code"        := RecCompanyInfo_L."IC Partner Code";
                RecPartnerAllocate_L."Cell Code"              := "Cell Code";
                RecPartnerAllocate_L."Location Code"          := "Location Code";
                RecPartnerAllocate_L.Quantity                 := RecItemLedgEntry_L.Quantity ;
                RecPartnerAllocate_L.INSERT;
              END;
              COMMIT;
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;
      EXIT(TRUE);
      // <-- 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteIcPartnersAllocation@1000000017(RecItemJnlLine_P@1000000000 : Record 83);
    VAR
      RecPartnerAllocate_L@1000000003 : Record 8053328;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Supprime les lignes de r'partition Boni/freinte pour la ligne Item jaournal line
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecItemJnlLine_P  : La ligne de la feuille de boni freinte . supprimer
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      WITH RecItemJnlLine_P DO BEGIN
        RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETRANGE("Journal Template Name","Journal Template Name");
        RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETRANGE("Line No.","Line No.");
        RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETRANGE("Journal Batch Name","Journal Batch Name");
        RecPartnerAllocate_L.DELETEALL;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckIcPartnersAllocation@1000000018(RecItemJnlLine_P@1000000000 : Record 83);
    VAR
      RecPartnerAllocate_L@1000000003 : Record 8053328;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Controle que la somme des qt's lignes de r'partition Boni/freinte = Qt' de la ligne Item journal line
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecItemJnlLine_P  : La ligne de la feuille de boni freinte . contr"ler
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      WITH RecItemJnlLine_P DO BEGIN
        RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Journal Template Name","Line No.","Journal Batch Name","Partner IC Code");
        RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETRANGE("Journal Template Name","Journal Template Name");
        RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETRANGE("Line No.","Line No.");
        RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETRANGE("Journal Batch Name","Journal Batch Name");
        RecPartnerAllocate_L.CALCSUMS(Quantity);
        // --> 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
        IF "Reverse Campaigns" THEN BEGIN
          IF Quantity <> -RecPartnerAllocate_L.Quantity THEN
            ERROR(Text8053007,"Location Code","Cell Code",Quantity);
        END ELSE
        // <-- 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
        IF RecItemJnlLine_P.Quantity <> RecPartnerAllocate_L.Quantity THEN
          ERROR(Text8053007,"Location Code","Cell Code",Quantity);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateIcPartnersItemJnlLine@1000000019(RecItemJnlLine_P@1000000000 : Record 83);
    VAR
      RecPartnerAllocate_L@1000000003 : Record 8053328;
      RecMCLocation_L@1000000002 : Record 8053083;
      RecSiloIcPartner_L@1000000001 : Record 8053335;
      RecLocation_L@1000000004 : Record 14;
      RecItemJnlLine_L@1000000005 : Record 83;
      RecItemJnlLine2_L@1000000006 : Record 83;
      TempJnlLineDim_L@1000000009 : TEMPORARY Record 356;
      cduPostItemJnlLine_L@1000000008 : Codeunit 22;
      CduCMCDimension_L@1000000007 : Codeunit 8053004;
      SiloManager_L@1000000010 : Boolean;
      IntLineNo_L@1000000011 : Integer;
      DocActionType_L@1000000012 : 'Create,Update,Delete';
      NextLine_L@60000 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cr'e les lignes journal article dans la soci't' gestionnaire par partenaire IC
      // Cr'e les lignes journal article IC pour les partenaires IC
      // appel' par le CU 23
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecItemJnlLine_P  : La ligne de la feuille de boni freinte . traiter
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // --> 20150909-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2092-FBE01
      {
      WITH RecItemJnlLine_P DO BEGIN

        TESTFIELD("Location Code");
        TESTFIELD("Cell Code");
        CheckIcPartnersAllocation(RecItemJnlLine_P);

        RecLocation_L.GET("Location Code");
        RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Journal Template Name","Line No.","Journal Batch Name","Partner IC Code");
        RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETRANGE("Journal Template Name","Journal Template Name");
        RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETRANGE("Line No.","Line No.");
        RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETRANGE("Journal Batch Name","Journal Batch Name");
        RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETFILTER(Quantity,'<>%1',0);
        IF RecPartnerAllocate_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          RecItemJnlLine_L.INIT;
          SiloManager_L := (RecPartnerAllocate_L."Partner IC Code" = '') OR
                           (RecPartnerAllocate_L."Partner IC Code" = RecLocation_L."Part IC Shar Silo Manager Code");
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Posting Date" := "Posting Date";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Document Date" := "Posting Date";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Document No." := "Document No.";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Entry Type" := "Entry Type";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Movement Type" := RecItemJnlLine_P."Movement Type";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Item No." := "Item No.";
          RecItemJnlLine_L.Description := Text8053008;
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Return Reason Code" := 'Inventaire';
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Shortcut Dimension 1 Code" :='';;
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Location Code" := "Location Code";
          RecItemJnlLine_L.Quantity := RecPartnerAllocate_L.Quantity;
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Invoiced Quantity" := RecPartnerAllocate_L.Quantity;
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Quantity (Base)" := RecPartnerAllocate_L.Quantity;
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Invoiced Qty. (Base)" := RecPartnerAllocate_L.Quantity;
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Gen. Prod. Posting Group" := "Gen. Prod. Posting Group";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Inventory Posting Group" := "Inventory Posting Group";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Unit of Measure Code" := "Unit of Measure Code";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Qty. per Unit of Measure" := 1;
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Product Group Code" := "Product Group Code";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Item Category Code" := "Item Category Code";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Cell Code" := "Cell Code";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Campaign No." := "Campaign No.";
          IF NOT SiloManager_L THEN
            RecItemJnlLine_L."IC Partner Code" := RecPartnerAllocate_L."Partner IC Code";
          CLEAR(cduPostItemJnlLine_L);
          CLEAR(CduCMCDimension_L);
          CduCMCDimension_L.DimFromItemJnl(TempJnlLineDim_L,RecItemJnlLine_L,TRUE);
          cduPostItemJnlLine_L.RunWithCheck(RecItemJnlLine_L,TempJnlLineDim_L);
          IF NOT SiloManager_L THEN BEGIN
            IntLineNo_L += 10000;
            RecItemJnlLine2_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecItemJnlLine_L);
            RecItemJnlLine2_L."Line No." := IntLineNo_L;
            RecItemJnlLine2_L."Shared Silo" := TRUE; // pour ne pas cr'er d''criture sur la cellule d'ajustement
            RecItemJnlLine2_L.INSERT;
          END ;
        UNTIL RecPartnerAllocate_L.NEXT = 0;

        IF IntLineNo_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
          RecItemJnlLine2_L.SETRANGE("Journal Template Name", RecItemJnlLine2_L."Journal Template Name");
          RecItemJnlLine2_L.SETRANGE("Journal Batch Name", RecItemJnlLine2_L."Journal Batch Name");
          RecItemJnlLine2_L.SETRANGE("Document No.","Document No.");
          CreateOutboxItemJnlAction(RecItemJnlLine2_L,DocActionType_L::Create);
          RecItemJnlLine2_L.SETRANGE("Document No.","Document No.");
          RecItemJnlLine2_L.DELETEALL;
        END;
        DeleteIcPartnersAllocation(RecItemJnlLine_P);
      END;
      }

      WITH RecItemJnlLine_P DO BEGIN

        TESTFIELD("Location Code");
        TESTFIELD("Cell Code");
        CheckIcPartnersAllocation(RecItemJnlLine_P);

        RecItemJnlLine_L.RESET;
        RecItemJnlLine_L.SETRANGE("Journal Template Name","Journal Template Name");
        RecItemJnlLine_L.SETRANGE("Journal Batch Name","Journal Batch Name");
        IF RecItemJnlLine_L.FINDLAST THEN
          NextLine_L := RecItemJnlLine_L."Line No.";

        RecLocation_L.GET("Location Code");
        RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Journal Template Name","Line No.","Journal Batch Name","Partner IC Code");
        RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETRANGE("Journal Template Name","Journal Template Name");
        RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETRANGE("Line No.","Line No.");
        RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETRANGE("Journal Batch Name","Journal Batch Name");
        RecPartnerAllocate_L.SETFILTER(Quantity,'<>%1',0);
        IF RecPartnerAllocate_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          RecItemJnlLine_L.INIT;
          SiloManager_L := (RecPartnerAllocate_L."Partner IC Code" = '') OR
                           (RecPartnerAllocate_L."Partner IC Code" = RecLocation_L."Part IC Shar Silo Manager Code");
          NextLine_L += 10000;
          RecItemJnlLine_L.VALIDATE("Line No.",NextLine_L);
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Posting Date" := "Posting Date";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Document Date" := "Posting Date";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Document No." := "Document No.";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Entry Type" := "Entry Type";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Movement Type" := RecItemJnlLine_P."Movement Type";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Item No." := "Item No.";
          // --> 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
          IF RecItemJnlLine_L."Movement Type" IN [RecItemJnlLine_L."Movement Type"::R10,RecItemJnlLine_L."Movement Type"::R11] THEN
            RecItemJnlLine_L.Description := Text60000
          ELSE
          // <-- 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
          RecItemJnlLine_L.Description := Text8053008;
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Return Reason Code" := 'Inventaire';
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Shortcut Dimension 1 Code" :='';;
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Location Code" := "Location Code";
          RecItemJnlLine_L.Quantity := RecPartnerAllocate_L.Quantity;
          // --> 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
          //RecItemJnlLine_L."Invoiced Quantity" := RecPartnerAllocate_L.Quantity;
          //RecItemJnlLine_L."Quantity (Base)" := RecPartnerAllocate_L.Quantity;
          //RecItemJnlLine_L."Invoiced Qty. (Base)" := RecPartnerAllocate_L.Quantity;
          // <-- 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Gen. Prod. Posting Group" := "Gen. Prod. Posting Group";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Inventory Posting Group" := "Inventory Posting Group";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Unit of Measure Code" := "Unit of Measure Code";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Qty. per Unit of Measure" := 1;
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Product Group Code" := "Product Group Code";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Item Category Code" := "Item Category Code";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Cell Code" := "Cell Code";
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Campaign No." := "Campaign No.";
          // --> 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
          IF RecItemJnlLine_L."Movement Type" IN [RecItemJnlLine_L."Movement Type"::R10,RecItemJnlLine_L."Movement Type"::R11] THEN
      BEGIN
            RecItemJnlLine_L."New Location Code" := "New Location Code";
            RecItemJnlLine_L."New Cell Code"     := "New Cell Code";
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Destination Movement Type" := "Destination Movement Type";
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Destination Campaign No." := "Destination Campaign No.";
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Cereal Retreatment Mvt" := TRUE;
            IF RecItemJnlLine_P."Reverse Campaigns" THEN BEGIN  // si quantit' cellule n'gative (les campagnes sont invers'es)
              IF RecPartnerAllocate_L.Quantity > 0 THEN BEGIN
                /// si quantit' partenaire positive on inverse les campagnes
                RecItemJnlLine_L."Destination Campaign No." := "Campaign No.";
                RecItemJnlLine_L."Campaign No." := "Destination Campaign No.";
              END ELSE
                /// si quantit' partenaire n'gative on inverse la quantit'
                RecItemJnlLine_L.Quantity := -RecPartnerAllocate_L.Quantity;
            END ELSE BEGIN
                /// si quantit' cellule positive et quantit' partenaire n'gative on inverse les campagnes et la quantit'
              IF RecPartnerAllocate_L.Quantity < 0 THEN BEGIN
                RecItemJnlLine_L."Destination Campaign No." := "Campaign No.";
                RecItemJnlLine_L."Campaign No." := "Destination Campaign No.";
                RecItemJnlLine_L.Quantity := -RecPartnerAllocate_L.Quantity;
              END;
            END;
          END;
          // <-- 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
          IF NOT SiloManager_L THEN BEGIN
            RecItemJnlLine_L."IC Partner Code" := RecPartnerAllocate_L."Partner IC Code";
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Shared Silo" := TRUE; // pour ne pas cr'er d''criture sur la cellule d'ajustement
          END;
          // --> 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Invoiced Quantity" := RecItemJnlLine_L.Quantity;
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Quantity (Base)" := RecItemJnlLine_L.Quantity;
          RecItemJnlLine_L."Invoiced Qty. (Base)" := RecItemJnlLine_L.Quantity;
          // <-- 20160513-Gen15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-FBE
          RecItemJnlLine_L.INSERT;
        UNTIL RecPartnerAllocate_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;
      // <-- 20150909-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2092-FBE01
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckSalesLineItemMix@1000000024(ICInboxSalesLine_P@1000000000 : Record 435);
    VAR
      ItemMix_L@1000000001 : Record 8053187;
      ItemMixRootItem_L@1000000002 : Record 8053188;
      ItemMixAddItem_L@1000000003 : Record 8053189;
      IsRootItemOK_L@1000000004 : Boolean;
      IsAddItemOK_L@1000000006 : Boolean;
      Item_L@1000000005 : Record 27;
    BEGIN
      WITH ICInboxSalesLine_P DO BEGIN
        IF "IC Partner Ref. Type" <> "IC Partner Ref. Type"::Item THEN
          EXIT;
        IF "Item Mix Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
          ItemMix_L.GET("Item Mix Code");
          TESTFIELD("Item Mix Root Line No.");
          IF "Item Mix Root Line No." <> "Line No." THEN BEGIN // Additive item case
            IsAddItemOK_L := FindItemMixAddItemSetup(ICInboxSalesLine_P,ItemMixAddItem_L);
            IF IsAddItemOK_L = FALSE THEN
              ERROR(Text8053011,ICInboxSalesLine_P."IC Partner Reference"
                               ,ICInboxSalesLine_P."Item Mix Code"
                               ,ICInboxSalesLine_P."Item Mix Rate");
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IsRootItemOK_L := FALSE;                      // Root item case
            Item_L.GET(ICInboxSalesLine_P."IC Partner Reference");
            ItemMixRootItem_L.RESET;
            ItemMixRootItem_L.SETRANGE("Item Mix Code","Item Mix Code");
            IF ItemMixRootItem_L.FINDSET THEN
              REPEAT
                IF (ItemMixRootItem_L.Type = ItemMixRootItem_L.Type::Activity) AND
                   (Item_L."Activity Code" = ItemMixRootItem_L."No.") THEN
                  IsRootItemOK_L := TRUE;
                IF (ItemMixRootItem_L.Type = ItemMixRootItem_L.Type::Family) AND
                   (Item_L."Family Code" = ItemMixRootItem_L."No.") THEN
                  IsRootItemOK_L := TRUE;
                IF (ItemMixRootItem_L.Type = ItemMixRootItem_L.Type::"Sub-family") AND
                   (Item_L."Sub-Family Code" = ItemMixRootItem_L."No.") THEN
                  IsRootItemOK_L := TRUE;
                IF (ItemMixRootItem_L.Type = ItemMixRootItem_L.Type::Species) AND
                   (Item_L."Species Code" = ItemMixRootItem_L."No.") THEN
                  IsRootItemOK_L := TRUE;
                IF (ItemMixRootItem_L.Type = ItemMixRootItem_L.Type::Item) AND
                   (Item_L."No." = ItemMixRootItem_L."No.") THEN
                  IsRootItemOK_L := TRUE;
              UNTIL (ItemMixRootItem_L.NEXT = 0) OR IsRootItemOK_L;
            IF IsRootItemOK_L = FALSE THEN
              ERROR(Text8053010,Item_L."No.","Item Mix Code");
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateSalesDocItemMix@1000000025(SalesLine_P@1000000001 : Record 37;ICInboxSalesLine_P@1000000002 : Record 435);
    VAR
      SalesDocItemMix_L@1000000000 : Record 8053190;
      ItemMixAddItem_L@1000000003 : Record 8053189;
    BEGIN
      WITH SalesDocItemMix_L DO BEGIN

        IF SalesLine_P."Item Mix Code" = '' THEN
          EXIT;
        IF SalesLine_P."Item Mix Root Line No." = SalesLine_P."Line No." THEN
          EXIT;

        FindItemMixAddItemSetup(ICInboxSalesLine_P,ItemMixAddItem_L);

        INIT;
        "Document Type" := SalesLine_P."Document Type";
        "Document No." := SalesLine_P."Document No.";
        "Document Line No." := ICInboxSalesLine_P."Item Mix Root Line No.";
        "Item Mix Code" := SalesLine_P."Item Mix Code";
        "Item No." := SalesLine_P."No.";
        "Fixed Rate" := ItemMixAddItem_L."Fixed Rate";
        "Allowed Rates" := ItemMixAddItem_L."Allowed Rates";
        Rate := ICInboxSalesLine_P."Item Mix Rate";
        Selected := TRUE;
        Quantity  := SalesLine_P.Quantity;
        Description := ICInboxSalesLine_P.Description;
        "Unit of Measure Code" := ICInboxSalesLine_P."Unit of Measure Code";
        INSERT;

      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE FindItemMixAddItemSetup@1000000026(ICInboxSalesLine_P@1000000001 : Record 435;VAR ItemMixAddItem_P@1000000000 : Record 8053189) : Boolean;
    VAR
      SetupFound_L@1000000002 : Boolean;
      TmpCompare_L@1000000003 : TEMPORARY Record 37;
    BEGIN
      WITH ItemMixAddItem_P DO BEGIN

        SetupFound_L := FALSE;

        RESET;
        SETRANGE("Item Mix Code",ICInboxSalesLine_P."Item Mix Code");
        SETRANGE("Item No.",ICInboxSalesLine_P."IC Partner Reference");
        IF FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            IF ("Fixed Rate" <> 0) THEN BEGIN
              IF "Fixed Rate" = ICInboxSalesLine_P."Item Mix Rate" THEN
                  SetupFound_L := TRUE
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF "Allowed Rates" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                TmpCompare_L.Quantity := ICInboxSalesLine_P."Item Mix Rate";
                TmpCompare_L.INSERT(FALSE);
                TmpCompare_L.SETFILTER(Quantity,"Allowed Rates");
                IF TmpCompare_L.COUNT = 1 THEN
                  SetupFound_L := TRUE;
              END ELSE
                SetupFound_L := TRUE
            END;
          UNTIL (NEXT = 0) OR SetupFound_L;

        EXIT(SetupFound_L);

      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetSalesDefCategoryCode@8063084(SalesHeader_P@8053002 : Record 36) : Code[10];
    VAR
      DocCategory_L@8053001 : Record 8061330;
    BEGIN
      WITH DocCategory_L DO BEGIN
        RESET;
        SETCURRENTKEY("Sales/Purchase","Document Type","Default IC Category");
        SETRANGE("Sales/Purchase","Sales/Purchase"::Sales);
        SETRANGE("Document Type",SalesHeader_P."Document Type");
        SETRANGE("Default IC Category",TRUE);
        IF FINDFIRST THEN
          EXIT(Code);
        EXIT('');
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ReCalcReceiptWeight@1000000027(DocNo_P@8053000 : Code[20];ICPartnerCode_P@80530001 : Code[20];ReceiptType_P@8053002 : 'Receipt,Transfert,Resumption');
    VAR
      RecPostedReceipt_L@1000000000 : Record 8052995;
      RecPostedReceiptLines_L@1000000001 : Record 8053104;
      RecIcPartner_L@1000000006 : Record 413;
      CduStatusMngt_L@1000000004 : Codeunit 8052985;
      QtyLine_L@1000000003 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Recalcule les poids sur la r'ception c'r'ales enregistr'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - DocNo_P : N› de la reception c'r'ale valid'e
      //   - ICPartnerCode_P : Code du partenaire IC
      // --> 20150617-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01 : ADD Parameter
      //   - ReceiptType_P : Type de r'ception
      // <-- 20150617-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01 : ADD Parameter
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      RecIcPartner_L.GET(ICPartnerCode_P) ;
      IF NOT RecIcPartner_L."Recalculate Standard Weight" THEN
        EXIT;

      // --> 20150617-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
      //RecPostedReceipt_L.GET(RecPostedReceipt_L."Receipt Type"::Receipt,DocNo_P);
      RecPostedReceipt_L.GET(ReceiptType_P,DocNo_P);
      // --> 20150617-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
      RecPostedReceipt_L.Status := RecPostedReceipt_L.Status::Open;
      RecPostedReceipt_L.MODIFY;
      IF RecPostedReceipt_L."Weighing 2" <> 0 THEN
        RecPostedReceipt_L.VALIDATE("Weighing 2",RecPostedReceipt_L."Weighing 2")
      ELSE
        RecPostedReceipt_L.VALIDATE("Net Weight",RecPostedReceipt_L."Net Weight");
      // --> 20150617-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
      IF ReceiptType_P = ReceiptType_P::Receipt THEN BEGIN
      // <-- 20150617-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
        RecPostedReceiptLines_L.SETRANGE("Receipt Type",RecPostedReceiptLines_L."Receipt Type"::Receipt);
        RecPostedReceiptLines_L.SETRANGE("No.",DocNo_P);
        IF RecPostedReceiptLines_L.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN
        REPEAT
          QtyLine_L := QtyLine_L + RecPostedReceiptLines_L.Quantity + RecPostedReceiptLines_L."Poor Product Quantity";
        UNTIL RecPostedReceiptLines_L.NEXT=0;

        IF RecPostedReceipt_L."Standard Weight" <> QtyLine_L THEN BEGIN
          RecPostedReceiptLines_L.FINDFIRST;
          RecPostedReceiptLines_L.Quantity := RecPostedReceiptLines_L.Quantity - QtyLine_L+ RecPostedReceipt_L."Standard Weight";
          RecPostedReceiptLines_L.MODIFY;
        END;
      // --> 20150617-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
      END;
      RecPostedReceipt_L.MODIFY;
      // <-- 20150617-GEN15/001-CMC02.02.00-3054-FBE01
      CduStatusMngt_L.ReleasePostedReceipt(RecPostedReceipt_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE ReCalcDeliveryWeight@1000000029(DocNo_P@8053000 : Code[20];ICPartnerCode_P@80530001 : Code[20];DeliveryType_P@8053002 : 'Delivery,Transfert,Resumption');
    VAR
      RecPostedDelivery_L@1000000002 : Record 8053014;
      RecIcPartner_L@1000000006 : Record 413;
      CduSalesStatusMngt_L@1000000004 : Codeunit 8052987;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Recalcule les poids sur la livraison collecte enregistr'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - DocNo_P : N› de la livraison c'r'ale valid'e
      //   - ICPartnerCode_P : Code du partenaire IC
      //   - ReceiptType_P : Type de r'ception
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      RecIcPartner_L.GET(ICPartnerCode_P) ;
      IF NOT RecIcPartner_L."Recalculate Standard Weight" THEN
        EXIT;

      RecPostedDelivery_L.GET(DeliveryType_P,DocNo_P);
      RecPostedDelivery_L.Status := RecPostedDelivery_L.Status::Open;
      RecPostedDelivery_L.MODIFY;
      IF RecPostedDelivery_L."Weighing 2" <> 0 THEN
        RecPostedDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Weighing 2",RecPostedDelivery_L."Weighing 2")
      ELSE
        RecPostedDelivery_L.VALIDATE("Net Weight",RecPostedDelivery_L."Net Weight");
      RecPostedDelivery_L.MODIFY;
      CduSalesStatusMngt_L.ReleasePostedDelivery(RecPostedDelivery_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE MakeCollectDistribute@8053025(VAR RecPostedRcptHeader_P@8053002 : Record 8052995);
    VAR
      RecPostedRcptHeader_L@8053001 : Record 8052995;
      RptMakeDistPtdDistr_L@8053000 : Report 60021;
      CduStatusMngt_L@60000 : Codeunit 8052985;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Lance la r'partition du bon valid' quand toutes les analyses sont saisies
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecPostedReceiptHdr_P :  L'ent^te de la reception collecte
      // Sorties:
      //   - N/A
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // 20150521-CER143-TER01.01.00-359-FBE01
      IF NOT RecPostedRcptHeader_P."Not Usable IC" THEN
        IF NOT RecPostedRcptHeader_P."Receipt Distributed" AND NOT RecPostedRcptHeader_P.Calibration THEN BEGIN
          CLEAR(RptMakeDistPtdDistr_L);
          CLEAR(RecPostedRcptHeader_L);
          RecPostedRcptHeader_L.SETRANGE("Receipt Type",RecPostedRcptHeader_P."Receipt Type");
          RecPostedRcptHeader_L.SETRANGE("No.",RecPostedRcptHeader_P."No.");
          RptMakeDistPtdDistr_L.USEREQUESTFORM := FALSE;
          // --> 20150713-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-1966-FBE01      -
          RptMakeDistPtdDistr_L.CallByICFunction(TRUE);
          // <-- 20150713-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-1966-FBE01
          RptMakeDistPtdDistr_L.SETTABLEVIEW(RecPostedRcptHeader_L);
          RptMakeDistPtdDistr_L.RUNMODAL;
          // --> 20150713-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-1966-FBE01
          // --> 20150826-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-2153-FBE01
          //CduStatusMngt_L.ReleasePostedReceipt(RecPostedRcptHeader_P);
          // <-- 20150826-GEN15/001-TER01.01.00-2153-FBE01
          RptMakeDistPtdDistr_L.CallByICFunction(FALSE);
          // <-- 20150713-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-1966-FBE01
        END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCMCSetup@60000();
    BEGIN

    END;

    PROCEDURE CancelPostedDelivery@60001(RecInboxPostedDelivery_P@60000 : Record 8053340);
    VAR
      RecPostedDelivery_L@60001 : Record 8053014;
      RecICDocumentMatching_L@60002 : Record 8053305;
      CUPostDeliveryYesNo_L@60003 : Codeunit 8052989;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Annule la livraison collecte . partir de la boOte de r'ception IC
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecInboxPostedDelivery_P : livraison collecte de la boOte de r'ception IC
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      WITH RecInboxPostedDelivery_P DO BEGIN
        //Recherche de la livraison transfert . annuler dans la table de correspondance document IC
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Source IC Partner","Source Type","Source Document Type","Source Document No.");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source IC Partner",RecICInboxTrans_G."IC Partner Code");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Document Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document Type");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Document No.",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document No.");
        GetCompanyInfo;
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Destination IC Partner",CompanyInfo."IC Partner Code");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.FINDFIRST; // Pas de gestion d'erreur, la correspondance doit exister
        RecPostedDelivery_L.GET("Delivery Type",RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document No.");

        //Lancement de l'annulation
        //Neutraliser les dialogues avec l'utilisateur
        CUPostDeliveryYesNo_L.SetHideValidationDialog(TRUE);
        CUPostDeliveryYesNo_L.CallByICFunction(TRUE);
        CUPostDeliveryYesNo_L.CancelPostedDelivery(RecPostedDelivery_L);

        //Archivage de la livraison transfert collecte IC
        ArchiveDeliveryInbox(RecInboxPostedDelivery_P,RecPostedDelivery_L."No.");
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CancelShippingOrder@60003(RecInboxShipOrder_P@60004 : Record 8053471);
    VAR
      RecShippingOrder_L@60000 : Record 8053265;
      RecICDocumentMatching_L@60002 : Record 8053305;
      RecHandledInBoxShipOrder_L@60001 : Record 8053472;
      RecInboxBlockTransaction_L@60003 : Record 418;
      RecInboxBlockShipOrder_L@60005 : Record 8053471;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Annule un ordre de chargement . partir de la boOte de r'ception IC
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecInboxShipOrder_P : Ordre de chargement de la boOte de r'ception IC
      // Sorties:
      //   -
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // --> 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
      WITH RecInboxShipOrder_P DO BEGIN
        //Recherche de l'ordre de chargement . annuler dans la table de correspondance document IC
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Source IC Partner","Source Type","Source Document Type","Source Document No.");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source IC Partner",RecICInboxTrans_G."IC Partner Code");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Document Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document Type");
        RecICDocumentMatching_L.SETRANGE("Source Document No.",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document No.");
        IF RecICDocumentMatching_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          RecShippingOrder_L.GET(RecICDocumentMatching_L."Destination Document No.");

          //Lancement de l'annulation
          //Neutraliser les dialogues avec l'utilisateur
          RecShippingOrder_L.CallByICFunction(TRUE);
          RecShippingOrder_L.DELETE(TRUE);

          //Archivage de l'ordre de chargement de la boite de reception IC g'r'e
          RecHandledInBoxShipOrder_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(RecInboxShipOrder_P);
          RecHandledInBoxShipOrder_L.INSERT;

          //Suppression dans la table des correspondances document IC
          RecICDocumentMatching_L.DELETE;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          CLEAR(RecInboxBlockTransaction_L);
          RecInboxBlockTransaction_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Transaction No.","IC Partner Code","Source Type","Document Type","Document No.");
          RecInboxBlockTransaction_L.SETRANGE("IC Partner Code",RecICInboxTrans_G."IC Partner Code");
          RecInboxBlockTransaction_L.SETRANGE("Source Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Source Type");
          RecInboxBlockTransaction_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document Type");
          RecInboxBlockTransaction_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",RecICInboxTrans_G."Document No.");
          RecInboxBlockTransaction_L.DELETEALL(TRUE);
          // COMMIT; comment FBE
        END;
      END;
      // <-- 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
    END;
